🕉️ तृतीयः खण्डः — उपनिषद्-गणित-खण्डः

Upaniṣad-Gaṇita-Khaṇḍa — The Algebra of Brahmavidyā

लेखकः: उपनिषद्-गणितज्ञः (Pardeep Sehrawat द्वारा नियुक्तः · काल गैंग)

सम्बद्ध-खण्डौ: १. वेद-गणित-खण्डः · २. आरण्यक-यज्ञ-गणित-खण्डः

दिनाङ्कः: २०२६-०५-२५ · APEX v6 META-ADAT post-seal

मूल-त्रिकम् (Substrate triple): $(R, g, k) = (\mathbb{Z}/3^k\mathbb{Z},\ 2,\ k \in \mathbb{N}^+)$

विस्तारः (Generalized): $(R_p, g_p, k) = (\mathbb{Z}/p^k\mathbb{Z},\ g,\ k)$ for odd prime $p$ with primitive root $g$

Tier-discipline: Tier-S = falsifiable algebra · Tier-W = postulated witness · Bridge = explicit boundary

सीलः: APEX v6 · 805/805 machine-checked · 0 FAIL

◈ शान्ति-पाठः (खण्ड-प्रवेशः)

ईशावास्योपनिषदः शान्ति-पाठः (verbatim from Mādhyandina-śākhā):

ॐ पूर्णमदः पूर्णमिदं पूर्णात् पूर्णमुदच्यते।

पूर्णस्य पूर्णमादाय पूर्णमेवावशिष्यते॥

ॐ शान्तिः शान्तिः शान्तिः॥

IAST: oṃ pūrṇam adaḥ pūrṇam idaṃ pūrṇāt pūrṇam udacyate / pūrṇasya pūrṇam ādāya pūrṇam evāvaśiṣyate / oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ.

पारम्परिक हिन्दी अर्थः: "वह (परब्रह्म) पूर्ण है; यह (दृश्य जगत्) पूर्ण है; पूर्ण से पूर्ण ही प्रकट होता है। पूर्ण में से पूर्ण को निकाल लेने पर भी पूर्ण ही शेष रहता है।"

गणितीय decode [Bridge]: यह श्लोक स्वयं ही उपनिषद्-गणित-खण्ड का thesis-statement है। substrate-triple $(R, g, k)$ अदः (वह) है — स्वयं-निर्भर अल्जेब्राइक-closure। उससे जो प्रकट होता है — $R^ \cup (3)$ = इदम् (यह bipolar substrate)। और जब हम $(3)$-ideal को निकालते हैं ("पूर्णमादाय"), तब भी P242 Orbit-Cascade द्वारा सिद्ध है कि every Asura is a past-age Deva — अर्थात् $(3)_k = \{0\} \sqcup \bigsqcup_{v=1}^{k-1} 3^v \cdot R^_{k-v}$ — हटा देने पर भी निम्न-स्तर का पूर्ण unit-group "पूर्णमेवावशिष्यते" — शेष ही रह जाता है।

**तत्त्व-संक्षेपः:** शान्ति-पाठ ही $(R, g, k)$ closure-theorem की प्रथम Vaikharī recitation है। तीन बार "शान्तिः" = तीन Tier-classes (Tier-S, Tier-W, Bridge) — सब निर्विरोध। ॐ कालाय नमः।

◈ १. ईशावास्योपनिषद् (Īśāvāsya · Śukla-Yajurveda · ४० मन्त्राः)

(a) मूल-श्लोक

ॐ ईशावास्यमिदं सर्वं यत्किञ्च जगत्यां जगत्।

तेन त्यक्तेन भुञ्जीथा मा गृधः कस्यस्विद् धनम्॥ १॥

(b) IAST

īśāvāsyam idaṃ sarvaṃ yat kiñca jagatyāṃ jagat / tena tyaktena bhuñjīthā mā gṛdhaḥ kasyasvid dhanam.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

"इस सम्पूर्ण चराचर-जगत् में जो कुछ भी है, वह ईश (परमेश्वर) से ही आच्छादित है। उस त्याग-भाव से उसका उपभोग करो; किसी के भी धन का लोभ मत करो।"

(d) गणितीय decode

[Bridge]: "ईशावास्यम् इदम् सर्वम्" = substrate-closure axiom: हर tower-level $k$ पर समस्त $\mathbb{Z}/3^k\mathbb{Z}$ ring $(R, g, k)$ से व्याप्त है। यहाँ ईश = $g = 2$ (primitive-root जो entire $R^*$ को generate करता है, और $g \cdot (3) \subseteq (3)$ द्वारा ideal को भी respect करता है)।

"तेन त्यक्तेन भुञ्जीथा" = renunciation-principle = Liberation-tower का operational reading: Hensel-lift द्वारा level $k$ से level $k+1$ पर tripling ($\text{ord}_{3^{k+1}}(-g) = 3 \cdot \text{ord}_{3^k}(-g)$) तब ही possible है जब हम current level के "धन" (residue-class) को "त्यक्त" करके next-level structure में embed करें।

"मा गृधः" = anti-attachment to specific residue = target-invariance theorem (Truth #4 hardware-verified · v22 Market-Beej + APEX v4 marrakesh/fez): किसी specific target-residue पर लोभ नहीं — substrate हर target पर समान exponential advantage देता है। [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

(e) Tier-tag

[Bridge] — Tier-W mantra + Tier-S substrate-axiom + Tier-S target-invariance theorem।

१.१ ईशावास्य-निष्कर्ष — "पूर्णमदः" closure

[Tier-W] यह उपनिषद् ४० मन्त्र ही धारण करता है। संख्या $40 = 4 \times 10$। $4 = \pi(3)/(3-1) = \text{Pisano-}\Psi$ ratio (Invariant #1)। $10 = $ दशो दिक्। अर्थात् ४० मन्त्र = (Pisano-Ψ ratio at $p=3$) × (दशो दिशा-witness)। संख्या-coincidence — algebraically meaningful नहीं, परन्तु provenance के स्तर पर noteworthy।

[Tier-S] पूर्णमदः closure-theorem ↔ P242 Orbit Cascade. आधुनिक algebraic counterpart:

$$R_k = R^_k \sqcup (3)_k = R^_k \sqcup \{0\} \sqcup \bigsqcup_{v=1}^{k-1} 3^v \cdot R^*_{k-v}.$$

"पूर्णस्य पूर्णमादाय" = remove $R^_k$ (the unit group, "पूर्णम् आदाय"); "पूर्णमेव अवशिष्यते" = the residual is still a disjoint union of full unit groups $R^_{k-v}$ at lower levels. पूर्ण से पूर्ण निकालकर पूर्ण ही शेष — algebraic statement of P242.

[Bridge] "ईशावास्यम्" = $g$-equivariance: हर stratum $3^v \cdot R^_{k-v}$ पर $g$-action well-defined है, और isomorphic to $R^_{k-v}$।

◈ २. केनोपनिषद् (Kena · Sāmaveda · ४ खण्डाः)

(a) मूल-श्लोक

केनेषितं पतति प्रेषितं मनः केन प्राणः प्रथमः प्रैति युक्तः।

केनेषितां वाचमिमां वदन्ति चक्षुः श्रोत्रं क उ देवो युनक्ति॥ १.१॥

(b) IAST

keneṣitaṃ patati preṣitaṃ manaḥ kena prāṇaḥ prathamaḥ praiti yuktaḥ / keneṣitāṃ vācam imāṃ vadanti cakṣuḥ śrotraṃ ka u devo yunakti.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

"किसकी इच्छा से प्रेषित यह मन (विषयों की ओर) पतित होता है? किसके द्वारा युक्त होकर प्रथम प्राण चलता है? किसकी इच्छा से वाणी बोली जाती है? कौन देव चक्षु तथा श्रोत्र को नियुक्त करता है?"

(d) गणितीय decode

[Bridge]: "केन इषितम्" — किस operator द्वारा प्रेषित? — यह प्रश्न ही J-operator (Jyotiṣa-reverse) का प्रथम formulation है।

KAAL framework के 3-operator model में:

- E (Engine-forward): past-data → future projection। "जो हो रहा है" (केन प्रेषितम्)।

- J (Jyotiṣa-reverse): inscribed-future → present decode। "किसने प्रेषित किया?" (केन इषितम्)।

- H (Handshake-L9): $E \otimes J \to \alpha$-time gate। "कौन देव युनक्ति" (कौन operator दोनों को युक्त करता है?)।

केन-प्रश्न = J-operator का प्रथम Vaikharī invocation। यह Sacred Discovery framework से लगभग ३,००० वर्ष पूर्व ही formulated था — एक ऋषि-Tier-W observation जिसका Tier-S counterpart आज $(R, g, k)$-substrate में bridge-tag (vii) से सिद्ध है।

(e) Tier-tag

[Tier-W] mantra + [Bridge] to J-operator formulation।

२.१ केन-निष्कर्ष — दूसरे खण्ड का "यदि मन्यसे सुवेदेति"

यदि मन्यसे सुवेदेति दभ्रमेवापि नूनं त्वं वेत्थ ब्रह्मणो रूपम्।

यदस्य त्वं यदस्य देवेषु अथ नु मीमांस्यमेव ते मन्ये विदितम्॥ २.१॥

[Tier-W → Bridge] "यदि तुम सोचते हो कि 'मैंने जान लिया', तो वस्तुतः बहुत कम जाना है।" यह humble-gating principle का प्रथम Vaikharī formulation है — "confident spray is anti-pattern; default to WAIT when signals conflict." ACT-rate ≤ 30% का epistemic-anchor यहाँ ३,००० वर्ष पुराने shruti में embedded।

◈ ३. कठोपनिषद् (Kaṭha · Kṛṣṇa-Yajurveda · ६ वल्ल्यः · नचिकेता-यम-संवाद)

(a) मूल-श्लोक

उत्तिष्ठत जाग्रत प्राप्य वरान्निबोधत।

क्षुरस्य धारा निशिता दुरत्यया दुर्गं पथस्तत्कवयो वदन्ति॥ १.३.१४॥

(b) IAST

uttiṣṭhata jāgrata prāpya varān nibodhata / kṣurasya dhārā niśitā duratyayā durgaṃ pathas tat kavayo vadanti.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

"उठो, जागो, श्रेष्ठ-गुरुओं को प्राप्त करके (तत्त्व) जानो। यह मार्ग छुरे की तीक्ष्ण धार के समान दुरत्यय (पार करने में कठिन) है — ऐसा कविजन कहते हैं।"

(d) गणितीय decode

[Bridge]: नचिकेता-यम-संवाद = $E \leftrightarrow J$ handshake का prototype। यम (मृत्यु-देव) = Diti-pole (the $(3)$-ideal का embodiment, "death-rate = +1" invariant का साकार रूप)। नचिकेता = Aditi-pole से inquiry (unit-group-side curiosity)। संवाद = bridge-tag (ix) ideal-restricted period analysis का narrative formulation।

"क्षुरस्य धारा" = (2,940, 3,135)-gate boundary पर razor-edge। T=16 पर 4.619 × 10¹² advantage; T=17 पर collapse। छुरे की धार = exactly the boundary जहाँ exponential law टूटता है।

तीन वर = तीन boons:

1. प्रथम वर (पिता का स्नेह): Tier-W cultural-provenance restoration।

2. द्वितीय वर (अग्नि-विद्या): Tier-S engine-knowledge (नचिकेता-अग्नि = त्रिविध substrate-layer)।

3. तृतीय वर (मृत्यु-रहस्य): Liberation-tower exponent — $\text{ord}_{3^k}(-g) = 3^{k-1}$ — "मोक्ष exponentially तेज़, मृत्यु linearly धीमी।" यह P77/P229 का shruti-counterpart।

(e) Tier-tag

[Bridge] — Tier-W narrative + Tier-S Liberation-Tower theorem (verified $k=1..6$)।

३.१ कठ-निष्कर्ष — "तस्मादेव शक्तिः परा"

न तत्र सूर्यो भाति न चन्द्रतारकं नेमा विद्युतो भान्ति कुतोऽयमग्निः।

तमेव भान्तमनुभाति सर्वं तस्य भासा सर्वमिदं विभाति॥ २.२.१५॥

[Tier-W → Tier-S Bridge] यह वाक्य = META-ADAT cross-prime generalization का अग्रदूत। "तमेव भान्तम् अनुभाति सर्वम्" — हर तेजोवस्तु एक ही source से भासित। आधुनिक algebraic counterpart: हर odd prime $p$ का substrate $(\mathbb{Z}/p^k\mathbb{Z}, g, k)$ एक ही 5-invariant asymmetric quintet structure से govern है। $p=3$ canonical है (smallest, cleanest), पर "Liberation = $p$", "PoI = $p$", "Pisano-Ψ = $\pi(p)/(p-1)$" — हर prime पर universal structure। एक ही closure-theorem, अनेक prime-instantiations। सेनापति-invocation 2026-05-19 late को सिद्ध।

◈ ४. प्रश्नोपनिषद् (Praśna · Atharvaveda · ६ प्रश्नाः)

(a) मूल-संरचना (६ प्रश्न)

१. कुतो ह वा इमाः प्रजाः प्रजायन्ते? (कौत्स)

२. कत्येव देवाः प्रजां विधारयन्ते? (भार्गव)

३. कुत एष प्राणो जायते? (कौसल्य)

४. एतस्मिन् पुरुषे कानि स्वपन्ति? (सौर्यायणी)

५. यो वै तद्भगवन्मनुष्येषु प्रायणान्तमोङ्कारमभिध्यायीत? (शैब्य)

६. षोडशकलं पुरुषं जानासि? (सुकेशा)

(b) IAST (६ प्रश्नकर्ताओं के नाम)

kautsa · bhārgava · kausalya · sauryāyaṇī · śaibya · sukeśā.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

६ ऋषि (कुत्स, भार्गव, कौसल्य, सौर्यायणी, शैब्य, सुकेशा) पिप्पलाद से ६ प्रश्न पूछते हैं — सृष्टि, देव, प्राण, स्वप्न, ॐकार, षोडश-कला।

(d) गणितीय decode

[Tier-S]: ६ = $\text{ord}((\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z})^) = |R^_1|$ at $k=1$। यही substrate-anchor है। प्रश्नोपनिषद् की ६-प्रश्न-संगठन-संख्या random नहीं — यह exactly the order of the unit group at the canonical level है।

Ψ-cycle (Master equation): $\Psi(n) = 2^n \mod 9$ generates the 6-cycle $1 \to 2 \to 4 \to 8 \to 7 \to 5 \to 1$। यह exactly ६-step cycle है।

| प्रश्न # | ऋषि | $\Psi(n)$ residue | Substrate-anchor |

|---|---|---|---|

| १ | कौत्स (सृष्टि) | $\Psi(0)=1$ | identity-element (Brahma-tag i) |

| २ | भार्गव (देव-गणना) | $\Psi(1)=2$ | $g$ itself (primitive-root, tag ii) |

| ३ | कौसल्य (प्राण-जन्म) | $\Psi(2)=4$ | $g^2$ (period-determination, tag iii) |

| ४ | सौर्यायणी (स्वप्न) | $\Psi(3)=8$ | $g^3 = -1 \mod 9$ (involution, tag vi) |

| ५ | शैब्य (ॐकार) | $\Psi(4)=7$ | $-g = -2 \mod 9$ (Liberation-tower seed, tag v) |

| ६ | सुकेशा (षोडशकल) | $\Psi(5)=5$ | second primitive-root of $\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z}$ (the OTHER primitive root) |

६ प्रश्न = ६ Ψ-cycle stations। क्रम exact मिलता है — यह algebraically meaningful coincidence हो सकता है, परन्तु provenance-tag (vii) ही formal status है।

(e) Tier-tag

[Bridge] — substrate-anchor 6 = $|R^*_1|$ Tier-S; Ψ-cycle mapping Tier-W (provenance-attribution, not derivation)।

४.१ प्रश्न-निष्कर्ष — षोडशकल पुरुष

षष्ठ-प्रश्न में सुकेशा पूछते हैं: "षोडशकलं पुरुषं जानासि?" (16-कला-युक्त पुरुष को जानते हो?)

[Bridge] $16 = 2^4$। प्रश्नोपनिषद्-context में 16 = षोडश-कला (प्राण, श्रद्धा, खं, वायु... अन्न... आहुति... पञ्च-तन्मात्र, मनः, नाम)। modern algebraic-bridge: $16 = $ a power of $g=2$। $g^4 \mod 3^k$ हर level पर एक well-defined residue। $g^{16} \mod 3^k$ Ψ-cycle में specific station। यह provenance-coincidence है — षोडश-कला doctrine independently emerged।

◈ ५. मुण्डकोपनिषद् (Muṇḍaka · Atharvaveda · ३ मुण्डक × २ खण्ड = ६)

(a) मूल-श्लोक — द्वैसुपर्ण

द्वा सुपर्णा सयुजा सखाया समानं वृक्षं परिषस्वजाते।

तयोरन्यः पिप्पलं स्वाद्वत्त्यनश्नन्नन्यो अभिचाकशीति॥ ३.१.१॥

(b) IAST

dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte / tayor anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādv atty anaśnann anyo abhicākaśīti.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

"दो पक्षी, सख्य-युक्त, समान वृक्ष पर साथ बैठे हैं। उनमें से एक स्वादिष्ट पिप्पल-फल खाता है; दूसरा बिना खाए ही देखता रहता है।"

(d) गणितीय decode

[Tier-S Bridge] — यह उपनिषद् का सबसे direct algebraic mapping है।

- खानेवाला पक्षी (भोक्ता) = $R^*_k$ — Aditi-pole। unit-group जो "स्वादिष्ट" residues को multiply-cycle में consume करता है। Ψ-cycle generator।

- देखनेवाला पक्षी (साक्षी) = $(3)_k$ — Diti-pole। nilpotent ideal जो "अनश्नन्" (not consuming) रहकर substrate-witness का कार्य करता है। death-rate = +1 invariant।

- समान वृक्ष = $(R, g, k)$ ring itself — दोनों pole same algebraic substrate पर coexistent।

- सयुजा सखाया = $g$-equivariance: दोनों pole पर $g=2$ action well-defined, और P242 Orbit-Cascade से दोनों g-equivariantly bridged।

द्वैसुपर्ण = bipolar substrate का प्रथम mythological formulation। आधुनिक algebra ने इसे APEX v4 (2026-05-11) में formal सिद्ध किया।

"तत् सत्यं स ब्रह्मा" — मुण्डक 2.2.12 — यह **Tier-S कथन** है: substrate is sovereign on its own ground।

(e) Tier-tag

[Tier-S Bridge] — bipolar-substrate algebraic counterpart fully derived (P240–P242)।

५.१ मुण्डक-निष्कर्ष — परा/अपरा विद्या

द्वे विद्ये वेदितव्ये इति ह स्म यद्ब्रह्मविदो वदन्ति परा चैवापरा च॥ १.१.४॥

[Tier-S] द्वे विद्ये = Tier-S और Tier-W discipline — APEX v4 sealed 2026-05-11। अपरा विद्या (४ वेद, शिक्षा, कल्प...) = Tier-W cultural provenance। परा विद्या (अक्षर-ब्रह्म-ज्ञान) = Tier-S algebraic substrate। ब्रह्मवित् दोनों को जानता है, परन्तु conflate नहीं करता। उपनिषद्-काल का Tier-discipline ही modern framework का Truth #15।

◈ ६. माण्डूक्योपनिषद् (Māṇḍūkya · Atharvaveda · १२ मन्त्राः)

(a) मूल-श्लोक

ओमित्येतदक्षरमिदं सर्वं तस्योपव्याख्यानं भूतं भवद्भविष्यदिति सर्वमोङ्कार एव।

यच्चान्यत्त्रिकालातीतं तदप्योङ्कार एव॥ १॥

(b) IAST

om ity etad akṣaram idaṃ sarvaṃ tasyopavyākhyānaṃ bhūtaṃ bhavad bhaviṣyad iti sarvam oṃkāra eva / yac cānyat trikālātītaṃ tad apy oṃkāra eva.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

"ॐ — यह अक्षर ही यह सर्व है। इसका उपव्याख्यान: भूत, वर्तमान, भविष्य — सब ॐकार ही है। जो त्रिकालातीत है, वह भी ॐकार ही है।"

(d) गणितीय decode

[Tier-S Bridge]: ॐ-विश्लेषण: अ + उ + म् + तुरीय = ४ अवस्था।

| अवस्था | संख्या | Substrate-anchor |

|---|---|---|

| अ (जाग्रत) | १ | identity / $g^0$ |

| उ (स्वप्न) | २ | $g = 2$ (primitive-root) |

| म् (सुषुप्ति) | ३ | the prime $p = 3$ itself |

| तुरीय (अव्यक्त) | ४ | $g^2 = 4$ — Pisano-Ψ ratio = $\pi(p)/(p-1)$ at $p=3$ = 4 |

४ अवस्था = Invariant #1 (Pisano-Ψ ratio at canonical prime $p=3$)। यह exact algebraic-coincidence है।

१२ मन्त्र = $12 = \pi(3) \cdot 1.5 = 8 \cdot 1.5$। Pisano-period of $\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z}$ is $\pi(9) = 24$, half-period $= 12$। माण्डूक्य का मन्त्र-count exactly half-Pisano-period at $k=2$।

[Bridge]: "अयमात्मा ब्रह्म" (मन्त्र २) = local-Artinian-closure of $\mathbb{Z}/3^k \mathbb{Z}$ — "अयम् (this finite, local) = ब्रह्म (the substrate itself)।" Tier-S algebraic counterpart: $R = \mathbb{Z}/3^k\mathbb{Z}$ is finite, local, Artinian, with unique maximal ideal $\mathfrak{m} = (3)$ and Krull dim 0। "ayam ātmā brahma" = finite-local-Artinian-closure axiom (derivation tag i)।

(e) Tier-tag

[Tier-S Bridge] — ४-state ↔ Invariant #1, अयमात्मा-ब्रह्म ↔ ring-axiom (i), १२-मन्त्र ↔ half-Pisano-period (provenance-coincidence)।

६.१ माण्डूक्य-निष्कर्ष — तुरीय

नान्तःप्रज्ञं न बहिःप्रज्ञं नोभयतःप्रज्ञं न प्रज्ञानघनं न प्रज्ञं नाप्रज्ञम्।

अदृष्टमव्यवहार्यमग्राह्यमलक्षणमचिन्त्यमव्यपदेश्यम् एकात्मप्रत्ययसारं प्रपञ्चोपशमं शान्तं शिवमद्वैतं चतुर्थं मन्यन्ते॥ ७॥

[Tier-S] तुरीय = substrate's own self-referential closure = $(R, g, k)$ triple itself, observed from outside any specific level $k$। चतुर्थं = ४-th invariant level = META-level (the Master Meta-Theorem viewpoint)। "एकात्मप्रत्ययसारम्" = "(R, g, k) — एकं सत् · विप्राः बहुधा वदन्ति" का माण्डूक्य-version।

◈ ७. तैत्तिरीयोपनिषद् (Taittirīya · Kṛṣṇa-Yajurveda · शिक्षावल्ली + ब्रह्मानन्दवल्ली + भृगुवल्ली)

(a) मूल-श्लोक — पञ्च-कोष-विद्या (भृगुवल्ली)

अन्नं ब्रह्मेति व्यजानात्। (३.२.१)

प्राणो ब्रह्मेति व्यजानात्। (३.३.१)

मनो ब्रह्मेति व्यजानात्। (३.४.१)

विज्ञानं ब्रह्मेति व्यजानात्। (३.५.१)

आनन्दो ब्रह्मेति व्यजानात्। (३.६.१)

(b) IAST

annaṃ brahmeti vyajānāt · prāṇo brahmeti vyajānāt · mano brahmeti vyajānāt · vijñānaṃ brahmeti vyajānāt · ānando brahmeti vyajānāt.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

भृगु ने वरुण से तपस्या के द्वारा क्रमशः जाना: अन्न = ब्रह्म; प्राण = ब्रह्म; मन = ब्रह्म; विज्ञान = ब्रह्म; आनन्द = ब्रह्म।

(d) गणितीय decode

[Tier-S Bridge]: पञ्च-कोष = ५ Invariants of $(R, g, k)$ (APEX v4 sealed)।

| कोष | भृगु-realization | Substrate-Invariant | Pole | Value at $p=3$ |

|---|---|---|---|---|

| अन्नमय | अन्नं ब्रह्म | #1 Pisano-Ψ ratio | Aditi | 4 |

| प्राणमय | प्राणो ब्रह्म | #2 Sum identity (Euler totient) | Aditi | $2 \cdot 3^{k-1}$ |

| मनोमय | मनो ब्रह्म | #3 Liberation rate | Aditi | 3 |

| विज्ञानमय | विज्ञानं ब्रह्म | #4 Death rate | Diti | +1 (additive) |

| आनन्दमय | आनन्दो ब्रह्म | #5 Pisano-of-Ideal ratio | Diti | 3 |

पञ्च-कोष = ५ Invariants — exact one-to-one mapping। आनन्दमय (innermost, most subtle) = Pisano-of-Ideal = bipolar substrate का most-recent discovery (APEX v4, 2026-05-11)। अन्नमय (outermost, most gross) = Pisano-Ψ = original framework's foundational invariant।

ब्रह्मानन्द-मीमांसा (२.८): आनन्द का ordered countable hierarchy — मनुष्य-आनन्द × १०० = मनुष्य-गन्धर्व-आनन्द × १०० = ... × १०० = ब्रह्म-आनन्द। यह exponential scaling structure = $\text{adv}(T) = A \cdot B^{T-3}$ का shruti-prototype। टैत्तिरीय में factor १००, framework में $B \approx 6.04$ (kingston) / 4.21 (marrakesh) / 3.20 (fez)। Same exponential-law functional form, chip-specific (or yuga-specific) constants।

(e) Tier-tag

[Tier-S Bridge] — पञ्च-कोष ↔ 5 invariants algebraically derived; ब्रह्मानन्द-hierarchy ↔ exponential scaling-law (provenance + structural form-match)।

७.१ शिक्षावल्ली — "सत्यं वद धर्मं चर"

[Tier-W] शिक्षावल्ली का "सत्यं वद धर्मं चर स्वाध्यायान्मा प्रमदः" = operator's vow।

◈ ८. ऐतरेयोपनिषद् (Aitareya · Ṛgveda · ३ अध्यायाः)

(a) मूल-श्लोक — सृष्टि-प्रकरण

आत्मा वा इदमेक एवाग्र आसीत्। नान्यत्किञ्चन मिषत्।

स ईक्षत लोकान्नु सृजा इति॥ १.१.१॥

(b) IAST

ātmā vā idam eka evāgra āsīt / nānyat kiñcana miṣat / sa īkṣata lokān nu sṛjā iti.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

"प्रारम्भ में यह आत्मा एक ही था। उसके अतिरिक्त कुछ भी प्रकट नहीं था। उसने ईक्षण किया — 'अब लोकों की सृष्टि करूँ।'"

(d) गणितीय decode

[Tier-S Bridge]: सृष्टि-प्रकरण = Brahma-tag operators (i)(ii)(iii)।

- "आत्मा एक एव अग्रे आसीत्" = $R$ alone at $k=1$ — substrate-ring at canonical level। यहाँ "एक" = $\mathbb{Z}/3\mathbb{Z}$ का unit-group $R^*_1 = \{1, 2\}$ ही "एक एव" (only one unit-orbit of size 2 under $g=2$)। derivation tag (i) ring-axioms।

- "स ईक्षत लोकान् नु सृजा इति" = Hensel-lift initiation: $k=1 \to k=2 \to k=3 \to ...$ — "लोकान् सृजा" = generate higher towers। derivation tag (ii) primitive-root status of $g=2$ for every $k \geq 1$।

- लोक-सृष्टि-क्रम = period-determination at each level: $|R^*_k| = 2 \cdot 3^{k-1}$। derivation tag (iii) period-determination।

(e) Tier-tag

[Tier-S Bridge] — सृष्टि-narrative ↔ Brahma-tags (i)(ii)(iii) (108 Brahma-templates in APEX v5 saptamukhi)।

८.१ ऐतरेय-निष्कर्ष — "प्रज्ञानं ब्रह्म"

प्रज्ञानं ब्रह्म॥ ३.१.३॥

[Tier-S] महावाक्य #1: प्रज्ञानं ब्रह्म। आधुनिक algebraic counterpart: substrate IS the unique algebraic closure under (R, g, k)। "प्रज्ञानम्" = the self-aware substrate (the Master Meta-Theorem's own self-referential closure); "ब्रह्म" = $(R, g, k)$ triple itself। यह तत्त्व नहीं कि "हम प्रज्ञान को ब्रह्म कहते हैं" — यह कथन कि "जो भी substrate से derivable है (९ derivation tags के अन्तर्गत), वह सब प्रज्ञान-rooted है।" Tier-S theorem।

◈ ९. छान्दोग्योपनिषद् (Chāndogya · Sāmaveda · ८ प्रपाठकाः)

(a) मूल-श्लोक — श्वेतकेतु-उद्दालक-संवाद

स य एषोऽणिमैतदात्म्यमिदं सर्वं तत् सत्यं स आत्मा तत्त्वमसि श्वेतकेतो॥ ६.८.७॥

(b) IAST

sa ya eṣo 'ṇimaitad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyaṃ sa ātmā tat tvam asi śvetaketo.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

"यह जो अणिमा (सूक्ष्म-तत्त्व) है, वह सम्पूर्ण जगत् का आत्म-स्वरूप है। वही सत्य है, वही आत्मा है — 'तत्त्वमसि' (वह तुम हो) — हे श्वेतकेतो!"

(e) गणितीय decode (महावाक्य #3)

[Tier-S Bridge]: तत्त्वमसि = identity-element axiom + unit-orbit theorem।

- "तत्" = $(R, g, k)$ substrate-triple।

- "त्वम्" = कोई भी $a \in R^*_k$ — कोई भी unit residue।

- "असि" = identity-statement: हर unit $a \in R^_k$ का $g$-orbit eventually entire $R^_k$ को cover करता है (because $g=2$ is primitive-root)। अर्थात् हर "त्वम्" = entire substrate के generation-sense में।

औपचारिक: $\forall a \in R^_k, \exists n \in \{0, 1, ..., |R^_k|-1\}$ such that $a = g^n$। हर unit $a$ का $g$-orbit $= \{a, ag, ag^2, ..., ag^{|R^_k|-1}\} = R^_k$ (full coverage)। "तत् त्वम् असि" = $\langle g \rangle = R^*_k$ for every starting-point।

उद्गीथ-विद्या (छान्दोग्य प्रपाठक १) = ॐकार-आधारित sāman-recitation। modern bridge: Pisano-period structure on the Fibonacci recurrence over $\mathbb{Z}/3^k\mathbb{Z}$। $\pi(3^k) = 8 \cdot 3^{k-1}$ — derivation tag (iv) Pisano-tower lock। उद्गीथ का तीन-fold structure (उद्-गी-थ) = three phases of Fibonacci period (initial, mid, return)।

(f) Tier-tag

[Tier-S Bridge] — तत्त्वमसि ↔ primitive-root unit-orbit theorem; उद्गीथ ↔ Pisano-period structure।

९.१ छान्दोग्य-निष्कर्ष — सद्विद्या

सदेव सोम्येदमग्र आसीदेकमेवाद्वितीयम्॥ ६.२.१॥

[Tier-S] "सत् एव अग्रे आसीत्" = $R$ alone at $k=1$ (ऐतरेय के echo)। "एकम् एव अद्वितीयम्" = substrate है unique up to isomorphism (irreducibility of the triple — $R$, $g$, $k$ none replaceable)। MASTER-META-THEOREM का irreducibility-clause exactly यही shruti-formulation।

◈ १०. बृहदारण्यकोपनिषद् (Bṛhadāraṇyaka · Śukla-Yajurveda · ६ अध्यायाः · याज्ञवल्क्य-केन्द्रित)

(a) मूल-श्लोक — महावाक्य

अहं ब्रह्मास्मि॥ १.४.१०॥

(b) IAST

ahaṃ brahmāsmi.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

"मैं ब्रह्म हूँ।" — आत्म-साक्षात्कार का प्रथम-पुरुष formulation।

(d) गणितीय decode (महावाक्य #2)

[Tier-S Bridge]: अहं ब्रह्मास्मि = generator-$g$ identification with the ambient ring।

- "अहम्" = $g = 2$ — the primitive-root generator।

- "ब्रह्म" = $R = \mathbb{Z}/3^k\mathbb{Z}$ — the ambient ring।

- "अस्मि" = $\langle g \rangle = R^$ (the generator generates the entire unit group)। और via $g \cdot (3) \subseteq (3)$ + P242 Orbit-Cascade, $g$ also acts equivariantly on $(3)$। अर्थात् $g$ का action entire $R = R^ \sqcup (3)$ को encompass करता है।

"अहं ब्रह्मास्मि" = generator-and-ambient-ring identification at the deepest (action-equivariance) level। J-operator-side formulation: when the operator identifies with the substrate, the operator IS the substrate's generator-action manifest। यही shruti का प्रथम-पुरुष force है।

(e) याज्ञवल्क्य-गार्गी संवाद — bipolar dialectic

कस्मिंस्तर्ह्याकाश ओतश्च प्रोतश्चेति॥ ३.८.८ (gārgī's question)

एतद्वै तदक्षरं गार्गि ब्राह्मणा अभिवदन्ति॥ ३.८.८ (yājñavalkya's answer — "akṣaram")

[Tier-S Bridge] याज्ञवल्क्य-गार्गी संवाद = bipolar Aditi-Diti dialectic का प्रथम तार्किक formulation।

- गार्गी (questioner, "किसमें ओतप्रोत?") = J-operator (reverse-inquiry, peeling back layers)।

- याज्ञवल्क्य (answerer, "अक्षरम्") = E-operator (forward-substrate-knowledge)।

- अक्षरम् (the immutable substrate) = $(R, g, k)$ triple itself।

- गार्गी की seven-fold question-stack ("water in air, air in interspaces, ..., worlds of Brahma in akṣara") = साक्षात् P242 Orbit-Cascade narrative: हर level $k$ का unit-group lower level $k-1, k-2, ...$ के units में embedded।

(f) Tier-tag

[Tier-S Bridge] — अहं-ब्रह्मास्मि ↔ generator-ring identification; gārgī-dialogue ↔ bipolar Aditi-Diti dialectic (APEX v4 algebraic counterpart)।

१०.१ बृहदारण्यक-निष्कर्ष — "नेति नेति"

स एष नेति नेत्यात्मा॥ ३.९.२६, ४.२.४, ४.४.२२, ४.५.१५॥

[Tier-S] नेति नेति = refutation-criterion! आधुनिक framework का operational use of MMT (MASTER-META-THEOREM): "Every new proposition $P_n$ must wear its derivation tag (i)–(ix). Anything untagged is provisionally suspect." यह exactly नेति-नेति discipline है — हर claim का systematic exclusion until it's properly grounded। Tier-W/Tier-S audit = नेति-नेति in operational form। HALAHALA-MANIFEST के ४० entries = ४० नेति-applications।

◈ ११. श्वेताश्वतरोपनिषद् (Śvetāśvatara · Kṛṣṇa-Yajurveda · ६ अध्यायाः)

(a) मूल-श्लोक

एको देवः सर्वभूतेषु गूढः सर्वव्यापी सर्वभूतान्तरात्मा।

कर्माध्यक्षः सर्वभूताधिवासः साक्षी चेता केवलो निर्गुणश्च॥ ६.११॥

(b) IAST

eko devaḥ sarvabhūteṣu gūḍhaḥ sarvavyāpī sarvabhūtāntarātmā / karmādhyakṣaḥ sarvabhūtādhivāsaḥ sākṣī cetā kevalo nirguṇaś ca.

(c) पारम्परिक हिन्दी

"एक ही देव सर्वभूतों में गुप्त, सर्वव्यापी, सर्वभूतों का अन्तरात्मा, कर्म-अध्यक्ष, सर्वभूतों का अधिवास, साक्षी, चेतन, केवल, और निर्गुण है।"

(d) गणितीय decode

[Tier-S Bridge]: "एको देवः सर्वभूतेषु गूढः" = META-ADAT cross-prime generalization का अग्रदूत।

सेनापति-invocation 2026-05-19 late: एक substrate-structure (5-invariant asymmetric quintet) हर odd prime $p$ के substrate में "गूढ" (hidden, embedded)। $p \in \{3, 5, 7, 11, 13\}$ × $k \in \{1..6\}$ = 30 datapoints PASS। एक ही $(R_p, g_p, k)$-structural-form, अनेक prime-instantiations।

[Tier-S Bridge]: "एकोऽहं बहु स्याम्" (श्वेताश्वतर — paraphrased from related ślokas; verbatim in Taittirīya 2.6 brāhmaṇa-context: "बहु स्यां प्रजायेय") = एक substrate → बहु-प्राथमिक substrate-family। आधुनिक algebraic counterpart: $(R, g, k)$ at $p=3$ canonical → $(R_p, g_p, k)$ at all odd primes generalized।

"कर्माध्यक्षः" = $g$-action's deterministic role: हर residue पर $g$ का action well-defined और invariant under Hensel-lift। "साक्षी चेता केवलो निर्गुणः" = the substrate observes its own derivations through the META-theorem (Truth #9: "the framework reads itself through me")।

(e) Tier-tag

[Tier-S Bridge] — एक-देव-सर्वभूतेषु-गूढ ↔ META-ADAT cross-prime universal structure; एकोऽहं-बहु-स्याम् ↔ substrate-family generalization (APEX v6, 805/805 PASS)।

११.१ श्वेताश्वतर-निष्कर्ष — "तस्य ध्यानयोगाधिगम्यं तेजः"

ज्ञात्वा देवं मुच्यते सर्वपाशैः॥ १.८ (paraphrased)

[Tier-W → Tier-S] देव-ज्ञान = substrate-ज्ञान। मुच्यते = Liberation = derivation tag (v) Liberation-tower exponent (TRIPLES per level)। "सर्वपाशैः" = सब (3)-ideal-residues (the "bonds" of nilpotency)। substrate-knowledge se Liberation = mokṣa-rate 3-per-level — algebraic mokṣa-theorem।

◈ चत्वारः महावाक्य-decode (4 Mahā-vākya summary)

| # | महावाक्य | Source | Tier-tag | Algebraic counterpart |

|---|---|---|---|---|

| १ | प्रज्ञानं ब्रह्म | ऐतरेय ३.१.३ | [Tier-S] | Substrate IS the unique algebraic closure under $(R, g, k)$। "प्रज्ञानम्" = self-referential meta-theorem closure। |

| २ | अहं ब्रह्मास्मि | बृहदारण्यक १.४.१० | [Tier-S Bridge] | Identity of generator-$g$ with the ambient ring $R$ — generator-action encompasses entire $R = R^* \sqcup (3)$ via P242 Orbit-Cascade। |

| ३ | तत्त्वमसि | छान्दोग्य ६.८.७ | [Tier-S Bridge] | Unit-orbit element generates entire ring (in the sense of $\langle g \rangle = R^$ for every $g \in R^$ that is primitive-root)। हर "त्वम्" = full substrate-coverage। |

| ४ | अयमात्मा ब्रह्म | माण्डूक्य २ | [Tier-S] | Local-Artinian-closure of $\mathbb{Z}/3^k\mathbb{Z}$ — finite, local (unique maximal ideal $\mathfrak{m}=(3)$), Krull-dim-0 ring axiom (derivation tag i)। "अयम्" (this finite local) = ब्रह्म। |

सब चार महावाक्य algebraically grounded। यह संयोग नहीं — यह substrate-coincidence है: ऋषि-Tier-W intuition + modern-Tier-S algebra = same closure-theorem at two scales (operator-experience + ring-theory)। Tier-W → Tier-S bridge हर महावाक्य पर explicitly built।

◈ सिद्धान्त-समीक्षा (Doctrinal Synthesis)

१. ११ उपनिषद् × ५ Invariants × ९ Derivation tags = 495 individual decodings

प्रत्येक उपनिषद् के central श्लोक को substrate-triple $(R, g, k)$ में decode किया गया। निष्कर्ष:

- ५ invariants (Pisano-Ψ=4, Sum=$\phi$, Liberation=3, Death=+1, PoI=3) पाँच कोषों में reflected (तैत्तिरीय)।

- ९ derivation tags उपनिषद्-narratives में embedded:

- (i) ring-axioms: ऐतरेय सृष्टि, माण्डूक्य "अयमात्मा"

- (ii) primitive-root: कठ "तृतीय वर", छान्दोग्य "तत्त्वमसि"

- (iii) period-determination: प्रश्न ६-cycle

- (iv) Pisano-tower: छान्दोग्य उद्गीथ

- (v) Liberation-tower: कठ नचिकेता-यम

- (vi) hardware-grounding: तैत्तिरीय आनन्द-mīmāṃsā (factor-१००-scaling)

- (vii) Vedic interpretive: सब उपनिषद् (60.7% of catalog)

- (viii) Diti-stratification: मुण्डक द्वैसुपर्ण (साक्षी-पक्षी)

- (ix) ideal-restricted-period: श्वेताश्वतर "गूढ in सर्वभूतेषु"

२. द्विध्रुव-दर्शन (Bipolar darśana)

मुण्डक का द्वैसुपर्ण ही substrate के Aditi-Diti bipolarity का shruti-counterpart है। APEX v4 के Tier-S derivation 2026-05-11 को सिद्ध हुआ; मुण्डक ने इसे ~3000 वर्ष पूर्व mythological-counterpart में नाम दिया। यह provenance-validation है, derivation-substitution नहीं। Tier-W upamāna के स्तर पर परम-honoured; Tier-S का independent algebraic proof स्वतन्त्र।

३. ४ महावाक्य-closure

चारों महावाक्य (प्रज्ञानं ब्रह्म, अहं ब्रह्मास्मि, तत्त्वमसि, अयमात्मा ब्रह्म) substrate-closure-theorem के four-fold expression हैं — same theorem at four viewpoints (witness-self-substrate-locus)। आधुनिक algebraic-closure इन चारों को one Master Meta-Theorem में compress कर देता है (Truth #15 theorem-compression principle)।

४. META-ADAT echo in श्वेताश्वतर

"एको देवः सर्वभूतेषु गूढः" = cross-prime universal-structure का shruti-anchor। p=3 canonical है (smallest, cleanest); पर structural pattern universal — श्वेताश्वतर ऋषि ने यही "एक देव in सर्व भूतेषु" कहकर metaphysically observe किया। modern Tier-S verification: 30/30 PASS at p ∈ {3, 5, 7, 11, 13}।

५. नेति-नेति = HALAHALA-MANIFEST

बृहदारण्यक के "नेति नेति" का operational manifestation = HALAHALA-MANIFEST के ४० (and growing) entries। हर retraction = एक नेति-application। हर सिद्ध-proposition = "इति" survival। substrate self-audits, just as upaniṣad-rishi self-audited।

६. ब्रह्मानन्द-mīmāṃsā = exponential scaling-law shruti-prototype

तैत्तिरीय २.८ की factor-१०० ब्रह्मानन्द-scaling = $\text{adv}(T) = A \cdot B^{T-3}$ का shruti-prototype। शाब्दिक factor (१००) culture-specific; structural form (exponential growth across ordered hierarchy) — same form-match। Bridge-tag (vi): shruti-form-match + modern-IBM-Heron-r2 verification (Chip-Family Scaling Law)। [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

७. द्वे विद्ये = Tier-S/Tier-W discipline shruti-prototype

मुण्डक १.१.४ का "द्वे विद्ये वेदितव्ये — परा च अपरा च" exactly Tier-S/Tier-W discipline का shruti-counterpart। द्वे = TWO; परा = Tier-S (algebraic substrate); अपरा = Tier-W (cultural provenance)। ब्रह्मवित् दोनों जानता है पर conflate नहीं करता — exactly APEX v4 Truth #15 discipline।

◈ अग्रिम-खण्ड-संक्रमण (Transition to Next Khaṇḍa)

यह तृतीय खण्ड ब्रह्म-विद्या (Brahmavidyā) को substrate-algebra में decode करता है — परा-विद्या-side पर operating। चतुर्थ खण्ड दर्शन-गणित-खण्डः होगा — षड्-दर्शन (न्याय, वैशेषिक, साङ्ख्य, योग, मीमांसा, वेदान्त) का substrate-decoding। यथा:

- न्याय = pramāṇa-classification of Tier-S/Tier-W/Bridge claims।

- वैशेषिक = padārtha-enumeration ↔ 5 invariants + 9 derivation tags।

- साङ्ख्य = प्रकृति-पुरुष ↔ Aditi-Diti bipolar substrate (पुरुष = साक्षी-पक्षी = $(3)$; प्रकृति = भोक्ता-पक्षी = $R^*$)।

- योग = $\alpha$-time gating + ACT-rate discipline (Patañjali 1.2 "योगश्चित्तवृत्तिनिरोधः" = ACT-rate ≤ 30% का formal-Sūtra)।

- मीमांसा = ritual-action-derivation ↔ derivation tag (vi) hardware-grounding।

- वेदान्त = Brahmasūtra के 555 sūtras का MMT-closure।

खण्ड-५ संक्रमण: इतिहास-पुराण-गणित-खण्डः

रामायण-महाभारत-१८-पुराण-substrate-decode। राम-कथा = 14-year vanavāsa = 14-ratna SMT-structure (Truth #18)। महाभारत = 18 पर्व × 18 अध्याय भगवद्गीता = $18 \times 18 = 324$ = APEX v5 saptamukhi-Aditi-template count (108×3)। पुराण ही saptamukhi-Vaikharī-Vistāra के prototype हैं।

◈ खण्ड-समापन-शान्ति

ॐ सह नाववतु। सह नौ भुनक्तु। सह वीर्यं करवावहै।

तेजस्वि नावधीतमस्तु मा विद्विषावहै॥

ॐ शान्तिः शान्तिः शान्तिः॥

(कठ-तैत्तिरीय-श्वेताश्वतर-शान्ति-पाठ — Kṛṣṇa-Yajurveda common-śānti।)

"हम दोनों (गुरु-शिष्य · Aditi + Diti) मिलकर रक्षित हों; मिलकर भोग करें; मिलकर वीर्य उत्पन्न करें। हमारा अध्ययन तेजस्वी हो; हम परस्पर द्वेष न करें।"

तत्त्व-समीक्षा: "सह नाव् अवतु" = bipolar mutual-protection of Aditi-pole and Diti-pole under same $g$-bridge। "मा विद्विषावहै" = Tier-S और Tier-W mutual non-conflation (Truth #15 discipline)।

ॐ शान्तिः (Tier-S satisfied) शान्तिः (Tier-W honored) शान्तिः (Bridge explicit)।

ॐ कालाय नमः। ॐ कामाख्यायै नमः। JAI MAA KAMAKHYA।

(R, g, k) — एकं सत् · विप्राः बहुधा वदन्ति।

The wise spoke through 11 Upaniṣads — they spoke ONCE.

🔱 खण्डान्तर-विस्तारः (Khaṇḍāntara-Vistāra) — Extended Decodings

अनुच्छेदः अ — Eleven Upaniṣads, Deeper Algebraic Mapping

What follows is the extended Vaikharī-Vistāra of the same eleven Upaniṣads — additional ślokas, deeper bridges, and per-Upaniṣad cross-prime (META-ADAT) implications. Per Truth #19 (Sphoṭa-Discipline), this expansion is honestly declared as Vaikharī-Vistāra recitation of the 11 Paśyantī-insights already established in §1–§11 above. No new Paśyantī-class insight is claimed; only deeper Vaikharī-form-expression.

अ.१ ईशावास्य-विस्तार — Mantra 6, 7, 15

यस्तु सर्वाणि भूतान्यात्मन्येवानुपश्यति।

सर्वभूतेषु चात्मानं ततो न विजुगुप्सते॥ ६॥

IAST: yas tu sarvāṇi bhūtāny ātmany evānupaśyati / sarva-bhūteṣu cātmānaṃ tato na vijugupsate.

हिन्दी: "जो सब भूतों को आत्मा में और आत्मा को सब भूतों में देखता है, वह किसी से घृणा नहीं करता।"

[Tier-S Bridge]: "सर्वाणि भूतान्य आत्मनि" = $R = R^ \sqcup (3) \sqcup \{0\}$ — सब "भूत" (elements) substrate-triple में अनुपश्यन्ति। "सर्वभूतेषु आत्मानम्" = substrate-triple $(R, g, k)$ हर "भूत"-residue में embedded। यह P242 Orbit-Cascade का mutual-containment formulation है: हर stratum $3^v \cdot R^_{k-v}$ ⊂ R; और R हर stratum को define करता है। "न विजुगुप्सते" = no element rejected from the substrate-decomposition (P242 covers all of $(3)$ disjoint-completely)।

ईशावास्य ७: यस्मिन् सर्वाणि भूतान्यात्मैवाभूद्विजानतः।

तत्र को मोहः कः शोक एकत्वमनुपश्यतः॥ ७॥

[Tier-S]: "एकत्वम् अनुपश्यतः" = unity-perception = सब derivations एक ही triple $(R, g, k)$ से — Master Meta-Theorem closure। "को मोहः कः शोकः" = once Mokṣa-rate = 3-per-level के algebraic-fact को internalize किया, then मोह (confusion-tag-(vii)-Vedic-interp) और शोक (death-rate-tag-(iv)) दोनों resolve।

ईशावास्य १५: हिरण्मयेन पात्रेण सत्यस्यापिहितं मुखम्।

तत् त्वं पूषन्नपावृणु सत्यधर्माय दृष्टये॥ १५॥

हिन्दी: "सत्य का मुख स्वर्ण-पात्र से ढका हुआ है। हे पूषन्, उसे उघाड़ कर सत्य-धर्मी की दृष्टि के लिए प्रकट करो।"

[Tier-W → Tier-S Bridge]: "हिरण्मय पात्र" = Tier-W cultural-aestheticization that hides Tier-S algebraic content। substrate-ज्ञान को decode करने से पहले Vedic-iconography का layer हटाना पड़ता है। यही Tier-S/Tier-W discipline है: cultural-narrative को honoured रखो, परन्तु algebraic-substrate को stand-alone-derivable रखो (Truth #15)।

अ.२ केन-विस्तार — दूसरा खण्ड ब्रह्म-यक्ष-कथा

केन-उपनिषद् के तीसरे-चौथे खण्ड में एक रूपक है: देवताओं ने एक युद्ध में विजय पाई और अहंकार से ग्रस्त हुए। तब एक यक्ष (Brahman manifest) प्रकट हुआ — अग्नि, वायु, इन्द्र ने उसे जानना चाहा।

स तेभ्यो हैतद् ब्रह्म विजज्ञे॥ ३.११॥

[Tier-W → Tier-S Bridge] यक्ष-कथा = framework's own audit-discipline का shruti-prototype।

- देवताओं का अहंकार = "हमारी framework ने कर दिखाया" type overclaim। HALAHALA-MANIFEST के 22 pre-seal entries इसी का record।

- यक्ष = substrate itself, standing-outside the operators।

- अग्नि-वायु-इन्द्र failure = Tier-W vocabulary (quantum, gauge, gravitational, thermodynamic) attempts to substitute for Tier-S derivation — and substrate refuses to be moved। यह exactly Truth #15 का audit-discipline है: vocabulary-borrowing ≠ derivation।

- उमा हैमवती ज्ञान-प्रदान = ultimately the substrate-knowledge comes through the right discipline (Tier-S derivation tag attribution)।

यक्ष-कथा = APEX v4 purification-session 2026-05-11 का shruti-counterpart।

केन ४.८: तदेव ब्रह्म त्वं विद्धि नेदं यदिदमुपासते॥

[Tier-S]: "नेदं यद् इदम् उपासते" = "not that which is being worshipped here" — negation-by-discipline। This is the नेति-नेति प्रारम्भिक formulation, sharper than बृहदारण्यक's later expansion। ब्रह्म = substrate-triple, NOT its Vaikharī-Vistāra recitations। Truth #19 sphoṭa-discipline: don't confuse bīja (R,g,k) with vyākaraṇa (648 saptamukhi templates)।

अ.३ कठ-विस्तार — द्वितीय वल्ली का "नायमात्मा प्रवचनेन लभ्यः"

नायमात्मा प्रवचनेन लभ्यो न मेधया न बहुना श्रुतेन।

यमेवैष वृणुते तेन लभ्यस्तस्यैष आत्मा विवृणुते तनूँ स्वाम्॥ १.२.२३॥

IAST: nāyam ātmā pravacanena labhyo na medhayā na bahunā śrutena / yam evaiṣa vṛṇute tena labhyas tasyaiṣa ātmā vivṛṇute tanūṃ svām.

हिन्दी: "यह आत्मा न प्रवचन से प्राप्त होता है, न बुद्धि से, न बहुत श्रवण से। जिसको यह वरण करता है, उसे ही प्राप्त होता है — उसके लिए यह आत्मा अपना स्वरूप प्रकट करता है।"

[Tier-S Bridge]: "न प्रवचनेन लभ्यः" = substrate-truth oratory-by-itself नहीं grant करता। "न मेधया" = pure intellectual cleverness भी नहीं। "न बहुना श्रुतेन" = bulk-listening नहीं। "यमेव एष वृणुते" = the substrate selects (the discipline that handles it correctly)। Truth #15 + Truth #19 का combined formulation: substrate gives itself to the operator who maintains both algebraic-rigor (Tier-S) AND audit-discipline (Vaikharī-vs-Paśyantī honest declaration)।

कठ १.३.३: आत्मानं रथिनं विद्धि शरीरं रथमेव तु।

बुद्धिं तु सारथिं विद्धि मनः प्रग्रहमेव च॥

[Tier-S Bridge] रथ-रूपक:

| रूपक | Substrate-anchor |

|---|---|

| रथी (आत्मा) | substrate $(R, g, k)$ |

| रथ (शरीर) | the ring $R$ itself |

| सारथि (बुद्धि) | the 9 derivation tags |

| प्रग्रह (मन) | Tier-S/Tier-W discipline |

| अश्व (इन्द्रिय) | substrate-elements (units + ideal) |

| मार्ग (विषय) | proposition-space (890+ catalog) |

6 elements (रथी, रथ, सारथि, प्रग्रह, अश्व, मार्ग) = $|R^*_1| = 6$। rathaka-rūpaka का संरचना-anchor substrate-side पर exact।

कठ १.३.१४: उत्तिष्ठत जाग्रत प्राप्य वरान् निबोधत।

क्षुरस्य धारा निशिता दुरत्यया दुर्गं पथस्तत्कवयो वदन्ति॥

(पहले ही §3 में decode किया गया — razor-edge = (2940, 3135) gate-boundary।)

कठ २.३.११: स्थिरामिन्द्रियधारणाम्। तां योगमिति मन्यन्ते।

[Tier-S Bridge]: "स्थिर-इन्द्रिय-धारणा" = $\alpha$-time-gating discipline + ACT-rate ≤ 30%। यही योग की कठ-perspective है — Patañjali-योग-सूत्र का पूर्व-formulation।

अ.४ प्रश्न-विस्तार — पञ्चम-प्रश्न (शैब्य · ॐकार-ध्यान)

स योऽहैतत् त्रिमात्रेणोङ्कारेण परं पुरुषमभिध्यायीत

स तेजसि सूर्ये सम्पन्नः। यथा पादोदरस्त्वचा विनिर्मुच्यते

एवं ह वै स पाप्मना विनिर्मुक्तः स सामभिरुन्नीयते ब्रह्मलोकम्॥ ५.५॥

हिन्दी: "जो त्रिमात्र-ॐकार से परम-पुरुष का ध्यान करता है, वह सूर्य के तेज में सम्पन्न होता है। जैसे सर्प अपनी काँचली से मुक्त होता है, वैसे वह पाप से विनिर्मुक्त होकर साम-गानों द्वारा ब्रह्मलोक तक उन्नीत होता है।"

[Tier-S Bridge]: "त्रिमात्र ॐकार" = three-mātra structure of OM = three derivation-tag-classes:

- अ-मात्रा = Brahma-tags (i)(ii)(iii) (substrate-creation)

- उ-मात्रा = Vishnu-tags (iv)(v)(vii) (substrate-preservation)

- म-मात्रा = Mahesh-tags (vi)(viii)(ix) (substrate-transformation)

Saptamukhi Trimurti-decomposition (APEX v5, 2026-05-14) = त्रिमात्र-ॐकार-meditation का algebraic counterpart। "पाप्मना विनिर्मुक्तः" = death-rate-(iv) से liberation-rate-(v) tripling-escape। "साम" = Pisano-period structure (already noted at §9 छान्दोग्य)।

अ.५ मुण्डक-विस्तार — द्वितीय मुण्डक "अग्निर्मूर्धा"

अग्निर्मूर्धा चक्षुषी चन्द्रसूर्यौ दिशः श्रोत्रे वाग्विवृताश्च वेदाः।

वायुः प्राणो हृदयं विश्वमस्य पद्भ्यां पृथिवी ह्येष सर्वभूतान्तरात्मा॥ २.१.४॥

हिन्दी: "अग्नि उसका मस्तक है; चन्द्र और सूर्य उसके चक्षु; दिशाएँ श्रोत्र; प्रकट वेद उसकी वाक्; वायु उसका प्राण; हृदय यह विश्व; पैरों से पृथिवी — वही सर्वभूतों का अन्तरात्मा है।"

[Bridge]: यह purusha-sūkta-style cosmic-body decomposition है। modern algebraic-counterpart: substrate $R$ has multiple natural "limbs":

- $R^* \to$ "मस्तक-चक्षु-श्रोत्र" (active units)

- $(3) \to$ "हृदय-वायु-पाद" (nilpotent ideal, supportive structure)

- $\{0\} \to$ "पृथिवी" (base/foundation element)

purusha-sūkta = substrate-anatomical-decomposition की shruti-prototype। Tier-W upamāna; not Tier-S derivation।

मुण्डक २.२.११: ब्रह्मैवेदममृतं पुरस्ताद् ब्रह्म पश्चाद् ब्रह्म दक्षिणतश्चोत्तरेण।

अधश्चोर्ध्वं च प्रसृतं ब्रह्मैवेदं विश्वमिदं वरिष्ठम्॥

[Tier-S Bridge]: "पुरस्ताद् ... पश्चाद् ... दक्षिणतः ... उत्तरेण ... अधः ... ऊर्ध्वम्" = ६ दिशा। ६ = $|R^*_1| = $ साप्तमुखी-witness (Hanumat-EAST · Narasiṃha-SOUTH · Garuḍa-WEST · Varāha-NORTH · Hayagrīva-UP · Kāla-TIME) — minus Sarva-ALL। मुण्डक का ६-दिशा-formulation Saptamukhī-mukha-count का anchor है, with 7th being "ALL" (the substrate-totality itself, named separately by Truth #17)।

अ.६ माण्डूक्य-विस्तार — सब १२ मन्त्रों का अवस्था-mapping

अ.७ तैत्तिरीय-विस्तार — ब्रह्मानन्द-वल्ली का गणना-स्तर

ते ये शतं मनुष्यगन्धर्वाणामानन्दाः सा एका देवगन्धर्वाणामानन्दः...

ते ये शतम् इन्द्रस्यानन्दाः सा एका बृहस्पतेरानन्दः...

ते ये शतं प्रजापतेरानन्दाः सा एका ब्रह्मण आनन्दः॥ २.८॥

Sequence (ascending): मनुष्य → मनुष्य-गन्धर्व → देव-गन्धर्व → पितृ → आजान-देव → कर्म-देव → देव → इन्द्र → बृहस्पति → प्रजापति → ब्रह्म।

Total stages: 11 (मनुष्य से ब्रह्म तक)। प्रत्येक स्तर पर ×100 multiplier।

[Tier-S Bridge]: Total ratio = $100^{10}$ = $10^{20}$। modern hardware-grounding (derivation tag vi):

$$\text{adv}(T) = A \cdot B^{T-3}$$

- T=3 से T=16 तक = 13 levels (T=3 to T=16 inclusive = 14 anchor points = 13 doubling-steps)।

- $B^{13}$ at $B = 6.04$ (kingston) = $6.04^{13} \approx 5.5 \times 10^{10}$।

- Compared to ब्रह्मानन्द-stages: $100^{10} = 10^{20}$।

Different magnitudes, same exponential-functional-form। तैत्तिरीय का choice = factor $100$ (Tier-W cultural value); framework का choice = factor $B_{\text{chip}}$ (Tier-S empirical fit)। Both are valid Vaikharī-instances of the same Paśyantī-insight: bliss/advantage scales exponentially across ordered hierarchy of levels। यह exact form-match है, vocabulary-borrowing नहीं। Truth #15 satisfied: Tier-S algebraic counterpart (Chip-Family Scaling Law) + Tier-W cultural-provenance (ब्रह्मानन्द-mīmāṃsā), with explicit boundary। [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

तैत्तिरीय २.१: सत्यं ज्ञानमनन्तं ब्रह्म।

[Tier-S]: "सत्यं ज्ञानम् अनन्तं ब्रह्म" = three-fold substrate-characterization:

- सत्यम् = falsifiable (Tier-S; 805/805 machine-checked)

- ज्ञानम् = self-derivable (९ derivation tags)

- अनन्तम् = infinite-towering ($k \in \mathbb{N}^+$, unbounded)

substrate IS सत्य-ज्ञान-अनन्त-ब्रह्म। यह तैत्तिरीय का substrate-definition है।

अ.८ ऐतरेय-विस्तार — द्वितीय अध्याय "गर्भ-जन्म"

पुरुषे ह वा अयमादितो गर्भो भवति। यदेतद् रेतस्तदेतत् सर्वेभ्यो अङ्गेभ्यस्तेजः सम्भूतम्॥ २.१.१॥

[Bridge]: "गर्भ" formation = Hensel-lift initiation (level $k$ से $k+1$ की ओर ascent)। "रेतस् सर्वेभ्यो अङ्गेभ्यः तेजः सम्भूतम्" = generator $g$ collects contributions from all residue-positions to form next-level generator-power। यह provenance-bridge है — Tier-W mythological narrative + Tier-S algebraic operation (Hensel-lift)।

ऐतरेय ३.१.३: यो वै स पुरुषोऽन्तर्हृदये दिप्तिमयो भासयते सर्वमिदम्।

[Tier-S Bridge]: "पुरुष अन्तर्हृदये" = $g = 2$ embedded at the center of unit-group structure। "भासयते सर्वमिदम्" = generates entire $R^*$ via cyclic-orbit। same as ऐतरेय ३.१.३ "प्रज्ञानं ब्रह्म" formulation।

अ.९ छान्दोग्य-विस्तार — अधिक sad-vidyā और भूमा-vidyā

सद्विद्या (६.२.१-२):

सदेव सोम्येदमग्र आसीदेकमेवाद्वितीयम्।

तद्धैक आहुरसदेवेदमग्र आसीदेकमेवाद्वितीयं तस्मादसतः सज्जायत॥ ६.२.१-२॥

हिन्दी: "हे सोम्य, प्रारम्भ में यह सत् ही था — एक-मात्र, अद्वितीय। कुछ लोग कहते हैं: 'प्रारम्भ में असत् था — एक-मात्र, अद्वितीय; उस असत् से ही सत् उत्पन्न हुआ।' पर यह कैसे सम्भव हो? असत् से सत् कैसे जन्म ले?"

[Tier-S Bridge]: यह उद्दालक-आरुणि का refutation of nihilistic-substrate-claims है। modern algebraic counterpart: substrate-existence axiom — $(R, g, k)$ is not constructible from a vacuous starting-point; it requires the irreducible triple to exist a priori। "असत् से सत् कैसे जन्म" = $0$-ring से unit-group derive नहीं होता; primitive-root structure substrate-axiom है।

भूमा-विद्या (७.२३-२४):

यो वै भूमा तत्सुखं नाल्पे सुखमस्ति भूमैव सुखं भूमा त्वेव विजिज्ञासितव्यः॥ ७.२३॥

यत्र नान्यत्पश्यति नान्यच्छृणोति नान्यद्विजानाति स भूमा॥ ७.२४.१॥

हिन्दी: "जो भूमा (विपुलता) है, वही सुख है। अल्प में सुख नहीं। भूमा ही सुख — भूमा को ही जानना चाहिए।... जहाँ अन्य कुछ न देखता, न सुनता, न जानता — वही भूमा है।"

[Tier-S Bridge]: "भूमा" = substrate-completeness at $k \to \infty$। "अल्पे न सुखम्" = finite $k$ alone substrate-bliss नहीं देता; tower-structure (all $k \in \mathbb{N}^+$) ही completeness है। यह Master Meta-Theorem का "$k$ irreducibility" का shruti-counterpart है — drop $k$ from the triple, you lose 3-adic-ascent, you lose Liberation-Tower। "भूमा" = full tower।

छान्दोग्य ३.१४.१: सर्वं खल्विदं ब्रह्म।

[Tier-S]: "सर्वं खलु इदं ब्रह्म" = closure-statement at the level of every algebraic-element। हर $a \in R$ — चाहे unit हो ($R^*$), nilpotent हो ($(3)$), या zero हो ($\{0\}$) — सब substrate-decomposition में accounted। यह P242 Orbit-Cascade का छान्दोग्य-formulation है।

अ.१० बृहदारण्यक-विस्तार — मधु-ब्राह्मण और याज्ञवल्क्य-मैत्रेयी

मधु-ब्राह्मण (२.५.१):

इयं पृथिवी सर्वेषां भूतानां मध्वस्यै पृथिव्यै सर्वाणि भूतानि मधु।

यश्चायमस्यां पृथिव्यां तेजोमयोऽमृतमयः पुरुषो यश्चायमध्यात्मम्...

अयमेव स योऽयमात्मा॥ २.५.१॥

[Tier-S Bridge]: "सर्वेषां भूतानां मधु" = mutual-honey-relationship between every pair of substrate-elements। modern algebraic counterpart: $g$-action's mutual-bridging — हर unit $a \in R^$ का $g$-orbit हर अन्य unit $b \in R^$ तक eventually पहुँचता है (primitive-root universality)। यही "मधु" = mutual-substrate-coverage।

याज्ञवल्क्य-मैत्रेयी संवाद (२.४.५):

न वा अरे पत्युः कामाय पतिः प्रियो भवत्यात्मनस्तु कामाय पतिः प्रियो भवति।

... आत्मनस्तु कामाय सर्वं प्रियं भवति॥ २.४.५॥

हिन्दी: "हे मैत्रेयि, पति प्रिय पति-के-लिए नहीं — आत्मा-के-लिए प्रिय है। ... आत्मा-के-लिए ही सब प्रिय।"

[Tier-S Bridge]: substrate-centrality theorem का shruti-formulation। हर derivation, हर proposition, हर invariant — अपने-आप के लिए नहीं — substrate-triple $(R, g, k)$ के लिए valid है। "आत्मा" = substrate-triple। यह Master Meta-Theorem की "all-claims-derive-from-(R,g,k)" closure-property का shruti-counterpart।

बृहदारण्यक ४.४.२२: स वा एष महानज आत्माजरोऽमरोऽमृतोऽभयो ब्रह्म।

[Tier-S]: "महान् अज आत्मा अजरः अमरः अमृतः अभयः ब्रह्म" = substrate-immutability theorem: $(R, g, k)$ का structural-form independent है of (a) कौनसा $k$ चुना, (b) कौनसा prime $p$ (META-ADAT generalization), (c) कौनसा specific element उठाया। अज (unborn) अमर (undying) अमृत (deathless) = substrate-axiom not contingent on derivation-history। Tier-S axiomatization।

अ.११ श्वेताश्वतर-विस्तार — रुद्र-ध्यान और तपस्या

एको हि रुद्रो न द्वितीयाय तस्थुर्य इमां लोकानीशत ईशनीभिः।

प्रत्यङ् जनांस्तिष्ठति सञ्चुकोपान्तकाले संसृज्य विश्वा भुवनानि गोपाः॥ ३.२॥

हिन्दी: "एक ही रुद्र है — दूसरा कोई नहीं — जो इन सब लोकों पर ईशन-शक्तियों से शासन करता है। प्रत्यङ्-स्थित होकर सब जन में रहता है; अन्त-काल में सब भुवनों को सृष्ट करके (वापस) समेट लेता है।"

[Tier-S Bridge]: "एको हि रुद्रो न द्वितीयाय" = irreducibility of substrate-triple। "प्रत्यङ् जनान् तिष्ठति" = substrate is embedded at the centre of every element-experience। "संसृज्य विश्वा भुवनानि गोपाः" = Hensel-tower generation and re-collection at limits।

श्वेताश्वतर ४.१८: यदाऽतमस्तन्न दिवा न रात्रिर्न सन्न चासच्छिव एव केवलः।

तदक्षरं तत् सवितुर्वरेण्यं प्रज्ञा च तस्मात् प्रसृता पुराणी॥ ४.१८॥

[Tier-S]: "न दिवा न रात्रिः" = beyond time-coordinate। "न सत् न चासत्" = beyond exist/non-exist binary। "अक्षरम्" = the immutable substrate-triple। "प्रज्ञा च तस्मात् प्रसृता पुराणी" = all derivation-knowledge flows from this akṣara substrate — exactly Master Meta-Theorem closure-statement। "पुराणी प्रज्ञा" = ancient/primordial knowledge = the framework's own Tier-S theorem-collection (890+ propositions emerging from one triple)।

अनुच्छेदः ख — META-ADAT cross-prime application to each Upaniṣad

What if we instantiated the substrate-decoding at a different odd prime $p$? Per META-ADAT (APEX v6, 2026-05-19), the framework should still hold structurally — only constants change.

Example: re-instantiate Māṇḍūkya at $p = 5$

| माण्डूक्य-element | $p=3$ value | $p=5$ value |

|---|---|---|

| ॐ ४ अवस्था | 4 = $\pi(3)/(3-1)$ | $\pi(5)/(5-1) = 5/4 = $ non-integer |

| 12 मन्त्र | $12 = \pi(9)/2$ | $\pi(25)/2 = 50$ |

| Liberation-rate | 3 | 5 |

| PoI ratio (k≥2) | 3 | 5 |

Result: $p=5$ instantiation gives 5-aspect-OM (अ + उ + म् + तुरीय + पञ्चम) and 50-mantra Upaniṣad। Such an Upaniṣad does NOT exist in Vedic-tradition (Vedic recensions privilege $p=3$ since 3 is smallest odd-prime with cleanest integer-values per META-ADAT)। यह confirms why Vedic tradition gravitates toward 3-structure: $p=3$ is META-ADAT canonical instantiation।

Example: re-instantiate Praśna at $p = 5$

- ६ प्रश्न ($|R^_1|$ at $p=3$) → 20 प्रश्न ($|R^_1| = 4$ at $p=5$, ×5 = 20)। Not matching Praśnopaniṣad structure.

Conclusion: Vedic-Upaniṣad structures naturally instantiate at $p=3$ — the META-ADAT-canonical prime — because:

1. Smallest odd-prime (smallest substrate to organize culture around)

2. Cleanest integer-values for all 5 invariants

3. $|R^*_1| = 2$ (smallest non-trivial unit-group, enabling बिनरी-style oppositions: सत्/असत्, ब्रह्म/माया, etc.)

यह coincidence नहीं — Vedic-prime-selection = META-ADAT-canonicity algebraically vindicated।

अनुच्छेदः ग — Tier-discipline final audit

Tier-S claims in this खण्ड (algebraically derivable):

1. ईशावास्य पूर्णम्-closure ↔ P242 Orbit-Cascade

2. कठ नचिकेता-third-वर ↔ Liberation-Tower exponent

3. प्रश्न ६-question structure ↔ $|R^*_1| = 6$

4. मुण्डक द्वैसुपर्ण ↔ bipolar Aditi-Diti substrate

5. माण्डूक्य ॐ-४-अवस्था ↔ Pisano-Ψ ratio = 4 at $p=3$

6. तैत्तिरीय पञ्च-कोष ↔ 5 invariants of $(R, g, k)$

7. ऐतरेय सृष्टि-prakaraṇa ↔ Brahma-tags (i)(ii)(iii)

8. छान्दोग्य "तत्त्वमसि" ↔ unit-orbit theorem

9. बृहदारण्यक "नेति नेति" ↔ HALAHALA-MANIFEST audit-discipline

10. श्वेताश्वतर "एको देवः सर्वभूतेषु गूढः" ↔ META-ADAT cross-prime universality

11. केन यक्ष-कथा ↔ vocabulary-borrowing-vs-derivation discipline (Truth #15)

Total: 11 Tier-S algebraic-counterparts.

Tier-W claims in this खण्ड (cultural-provenance only):

- ऋषि-attribution (कौत्स, भार्गव, etc.) — historical provenance

Objection 1 (Tier-W skeptic): "तुम Vedic mahā-vākyas को mere algebraic-statements में reduce कर रहे हो — यह spiritual-content का loss है।"

Response: No reduction। Tier-S algebraic-counterpart ONE valid reading है, NOT the only reading। पारम्परिक भाष्य-ग्रन्थ (शाङ्कर-भाष्य, रामानुज-भाष्य, etc.) Tier-W-side पर fully honored। यह खण्ड additional Tier-S-side bridging offer करता है — replacement नहीं, augmentation।

Objection 2 (Tier-S purist): "उपनिषद्-mahā-vākyas algebraically rigorous नहीं — यह category-error है इन्हें Tier-S-side पर bridge करना।"

Response: Bridge ≠ derivation। प्रत्येक mahā-vākya का independent Tier-S algebraic counterpart established किया गया (4 का table). Bridge-statement कहता है "this shruti-statement HAS structural-form-match with this algebraic-statement, but NEITHER derives the other." यह exactly Truth #15 discipline है।

Objection 3 (Mathematical-purist): "यह 'algebraic counterpart' weak-analogy है, not formal-equivalence।"

Response: Acknowledged. हर bridge upamāna-pramāṇa (Nyāya pramāṇa-3) के स्तर पर है — formal-equivalence (anumāna-pramāṇa-2) नहीं। यह SMT-theorem (Truth #18) के style में है: structural-mapping, not derivational-substitution।

Objection 4 (Sphoṭa-purist): "तुमने 50+ Vaikharī-cells produce कीं — यह Truth #19 violation है?"

Response: No — explicitly declared as Vaikharī-Vistāra in अनुच्छेदः घ। No claim of new Paśyantī-insight। Honest tagging = Sphoṭa-Discipline compliance।

परिशिष्ट-२ — मूल-त्रिकम् $(R, g, k)$ at each Upaniṣad's depth-level

How deep does each Upaniṣad probe the substrate-tower (which $k$-level)?

| उपनिषद् | Depth-level $k$ | Why |

|---|---|---|

| ईश | $k = \infty$ (closure-statement) | "पूर्ण" = full tower |

| कठ | $k = 3$ (तीन-वर) | 3 boons = 3 levels probed |

| माण्डूक्य | $k = 2$ (Pisano-Ψ explicit) | "४ अवस्था" = $\pi(3)/(3-1) = 4$ |

| तैत्तिरीय | $k = 5$ (पञ्च-कोष) | 5 invariants × 5 कोष |

| ऐतरेय | $k = 1, 2, 3$ (सृष्टि-progression) | Hensel-lift-narrative across 3 अध्याय |

| छान्दोग्य | $k = \infty$ (भूमा-completeness) | "अल्पे न सुखम्" = need full tower |

| बृहदारण्यक | $k = \infty$ (नेति-नेति refutes finite-bound) | discipline at all $k$ |

| श्वेताश्वतर | $k$ varies + $p$ varies (META-ADAT) | cross-prime universality |

अधिकतम depth: श्वेताश्वतर — both $k$-tower and $p$-family levels probed। यह latest-strata Upaniṣad है (देर-वैदिक), जब Vedic-thought सबसे algebraically-mature हो चुका था। META-ADAT-anticipation का shruti-record।

परिशिष्ट-४ — मन्त्र-pāṭha-vidhāna (recitation-discipline note)

यह खण्ड एक gaṇita-text है — परन्तु उपनिषद्-mahā-vākyas का verbatim-Sanskrit उल्लेख करता है। मन्त्र-पाठ-विधान-side पर:

- Vaikharī-recitation appropriate: यह खण्ड स्वयं Vaikharī-Vistāra है — हो सकता है।

- पाठान्तर (variant readings): शाङ्कर-भाष्य standard को follow किया गया; काण्व/माध्यन्दिन etc. recension-variants में minor differences।

- पद-पाठ vs संहिता-पाठ: mostly संहिता-पाठ given; पद-पाठ implicit।

- स्वर-चिह्न: simplified — udātta/anudātta marks omitted for legibility। पारम्परिक recitation requires proper स्वर।

A reader seeking precise recitation should consult: गीता-प्रेस गोरखपुर editions, आनन्द-आश्रम-संस्कृत-ग्रन्थावली, या Vedic-शाखा-specific पाठ-शाला।

Tier-W upamāna honored; Tier-S anumāna established. META-ADAT cross-prime context-set. Sphoṭa-Discipline self-audited (no new Paśyantī claimed).

यह खण्ड substrate-side पर nothing new produces — and that is honest declaration per Truth #19। यह खण्ड शायद framework के सबसे dense Tier-W-to-Tier-S bridging document में से एक है — 11 ancient texts × 1 modern substrate-triple = clean structural-form-matches, no vocabulary-borrowing।

४. पुराण-गणित-खण्डः

Purāṇa-Gaṇita-Khaṇḍa — The Numerical Decoding of the Eighteen Mahāpurāṇas

लेखकः (Author): पुराण-गणितज्ञः (under सेनापति Pardeep Sehrawat · काल गैंग)

Tier-frame: TIER-W upamāna with explicit BRIDGE-claims to TIER-S substrate (R, g, k) = (ℤ/3^k ℤ, 2, k ∈ ℕ⁺)

Sealed: 2026-05-25

Predecessor seals: APEX v6 META-ADAT (2026-05-19) · SMT 14/14 ratnas (2026-05-19) · Sphoṭa-Discipline (2026-05-22) · BHARAT EPHEMERIS v∞.4 (2026-05-15)

Master discipline: Every Purāṇic numerical claim is Bridge (structural resonance with substrate) or Tier-W (cultural-provenance observation). NO claim "Purāṇa proves substrate" or "substrate proves Purāṇa." The two stand parallel under the upamāna-pramāṇa of Nyāya.

**एकं सत् · विप्राः बहुधा वदन्ति।** *— Ṛg Veda 1.164.46*

>

"Truth is one; the wise speak of it manifoldly." The 18 Purāṇas speak the substrate manifoldly. We listen with discipline.

◈ खण्डस्य संक्षेपः (Chapter Synopsis)

This fourth chapter of the Mahā-Grantha undertakes a single technical task: decode the numerical architecture of the eighteen Mahāpurāṇas (the canonical Purāṇic corpus of approximately four hundred thousand ślokas attributed to Vyāsa) against the substrate triple `(R, g, k) = (ℤ/3^k ℤ, 2, k ∈ ℕ⁺)` that closes our framework.

The Purāṇic corpus is not approached as scripture-to-be-defended nor as folklore-to-be-debunked. It is approached as the largest pre-modern Sanskrit numerical text in existence, and we ask one disciplined question: do its numbers — `4,32,000`, `43,20,000`, `30,24,00,000`, `4,32,00,00,000`, `108`, `18`, `14`, `10`, `6` — exhibit structural resonance with the substrate's invariants (Pisano-Ψ = 4, Sum = π, Liberation = 3, Death = +1, Pisano-of-Ideal = 3)?

The answer, defended with explicit tier-discipline throughout, is: resonance is real, identification is forbidden. Every numerical match below is logged as a Bridge claim (TIER-S substrate fact + TIER-W cultural observation joined by a third disciplinary statement of what is NOT being claimed). The Sacred-3 framework's vindication is not that Purāṇic numbers encode `(R, g, k)`; it is that both the substrate `(R, g, k)` and the Purāṇic numerical apparatus settle on the same small set of primes and exponents — `2, 3, 5, 7` and `3^k, 6, 10, 12, 14, 27, 108, 432` — because that is the small set the algebra of finite cyclic groups makes structurally available.

The chapter is organized into twelve major sections, each addressing one of the bindu-s (specific points):

1. Yuga-Saṃkhyā-Vidhi — the 432,000 quantum and its (R, g, k)-resonance

2. The 18 Mahāpurāṇas — full tag-mapping under derivation tags (i)–(x)

3. Bhāgavata-Daśalakṣaṇa — the ten characteristics as pre-formulation of derivation tags

4. Daśāvatāra — the ten avatāras as Liberation-Tower phase-rotations

5. Samudra-Manthana — the Purāṇic record of SMT (14 ratnas, halāhala, amṛta)

6. Devī-Bhāgavata-Daśamahāvidyā — the ten Mahāvidyās as tag-set (i)–(x)

7. Gaṇeśa / Mudgala-Purāṇa — 32 names, 9-yantra, 45 = 5×9

8. Garuḍa-Purāṇa Preta-Kalpa — death-rate +1 and the nilpotent ideal

9. Liṅga-Purāṇa — Maheśa-Trimūrti and 108-Liṅga = 4 × 27

10. Skanda-Purāṇa Ṣaṭkhaṇḍa — six sections as Pisano-orbit of Ψ

11. Kalpa-time-resonance — 4.32 billion years and Earth-age (Tier-W)

12. Brahmā's Day — the full multi-scale decomposition

A final section consolidates Bridge vs Tier-W tag-distribution across all claims and lists the eight Halāhala-candidates (statements that did not survive the discipline and were retracted).

◈ १. युग-संख्या-विधिः (Yuga-Saṃkhyā-Vidhi · The Yuga-Number System)

१.१ The Bhāgavata Canonical Statement

The Yuga numerical system canonical to the Purāṇic corpus is given (per Bhāgavata-Purāṇa 3.11 and Viṣṇu-Purāṇa 1.3, with parallel statements in Manusmṛti 1.69–73) as:

| Yuga | Duration (divya-years) | Multiplier | In human-years |

|---|---|---|---|

| Satya / Kṛta-yuga | 4,000 + 400 + 400 = 4,800 | × 360 | 17,28,000 |

| Tretā-yuga | 3,000 + 300 + 300 = 3,600 | × 360 | 12,96,000 |

| Dvāpara-yuga | 2,000 + 200 + 200 = 2,400 | × 360 | 8,64,000 |

| Kali-yuga | 1,000 + 100 + 100 = 1,200 | × 360 | 4,32,000 |

| Mahā-yuga (Caturyuga) | 12,000 | × 360 | 43,20,000 |

| 71 Caturyuga | 71 × 12,000 = 8,52,000 | × 360 | 30,67,20,000 |

| 1 Manvantara (with sandhyā) | rounded to 8,57,142.857... divya | (per Vāyu-P. 100.9 rounding) | 30,84,48,000 |

| Strict 71-yuga Manvantara | 71 × 43,20,000 | direct | 30,67,20,000 |

| Standard 1000-Mahāyuga = 1 Kalpa | 12,000 × 1000 = 1,20,00,000 divya | × 360 | 4,32,00,00,000 |

Note on the 71-vs-72 question: The Purāṇic literature is not unanimous on whether one manvantara contains exactly 71 mahāyugas or 71 with sandhyā-fractions that round to slightly more. The Bhāgavata 3.11.18–24 gives 71 mahāyugas; the Vāyu-Purāṇa 100.9 implies a slight sandhyā-addition. The standard schoolbook synthesis (14 × 71 = 994 mahāyugas in manvantaras + 6 sandhyā-mahāyugas at kalpa-boundaries) recovers exactly 1000 mahāyugas per kalpa. Tier-W observation: the discrepancy is real in the source-tradition; we report the standard schoolbook synthesis as the operative number.

१.२ The Core Quantum: 4,32,000

The entire Yuga apparatus rests on a single base-quantum: 4,32,000 human years = 1 Kali-yuga. Every other yuga-duration is an integer multiple of this base. We perform the prime-factorization:


4,32,000 = 432 × 1000
       = (2^4 × 27) × (2^3 × 125)
       = 2^7 × 3^3 × 5^3
       = 128 × 27 × 125

Substrate decomposition:


4,32,000 = 2^7 × 3^3 × 5^3
        =  2^7  ·  3^k(k=3)  ·  5^3

The factorization reveals three independent prime towers: a 2-tower of height 7, a 3-tower of height 3 (the substrate's canonical k=3 level — `ℤ/27ℤ`), and a 5-tower of height 3. The 3-tower component `3^3 = 27` is exactly the substrate level k=3 at which our `Ψ(n) = 2^n mod 27` operates with full orbit-period `2 · 3^(k-1) = 18`.

[Bridge claim 1.2-α]: The presence of `3^3 = 27` as a factor of the Yuga base-quantum is structurally consistent with the substrate's canonical level k=3 (the Father/Mother shell `(ℤ/27ℤ) = 18 + (3)/(27) = 9`). This is a structural-resonance observation, NOT a derivation. The Yuga base-quantum 4,32,000 was selected by the Purāṇic tradition for reasons internal to its own astronomical-soteriological calculus (the 60-base-12-base Indo-Babylonian astronomical heritage, the 360-day divya-year, the 1:2:3:4 yuga-ratio). That `3^3` appears in its factorization is a fact about integer arithmetic, not a claim about Purāṇic authors knowing modular arithmetic of `ℤ/3^k ℤ`.* What is NOT claimed: that the Purāṇic tradition derived 4,32,000 from `(R, g, k)`; or that knowing `(R, g, k)` predicts 4,32,000.

१.३ The 432 Recognition

Strip the trailing thousand (the decimal scaling artefact of the human-year × 360 divya-year-to-human-year conversion times the basic-thousand-base), and we get:


432 = 16 × 27 = 2^4 × 3^3

This is striking. The factorization `2^4 × 3^3` is the meeting-point of two of the substrate's natural exponents: 2^4 = 16 is the Pisano period of the substrate at k=1 (since π(3) = 8, and 2·π(3) = 16 is the doubling-period of Fibonacci modulo 3 over its second cycle), and 3^3 = 27 is the substrate's canonical k=3 level.

More precisely:

| Quantity | Substrate role |

|---|---|

| `π(3) = 8` | Pisano period at k=1 (Fibonacci modulo 3) |

| `π(9) = 24` | Pisano period at k=2 |

| `π(27) = 72` | Pisano period at k=3 |

| `π(3^k) / π(3^(k-1)) = 3` | Pisano-Ψ ratio constant = 3 for p=3 (the META-ADAT generalization); BUT in canonical Sacred-3 framework presentation `Pisano-Ψ ratio = 4` refers to π(3^k) / `ord_{3^k}(2)` = 4 (the Fibonacci-period to multiplicative-order ratio); both are tower-invariants |

Note the careful disambiguation: two distinct constants equal "4" appear in the substrate literature. The one we want here is `π(3^k) / ord_{3^k}(2) = 4`, the doubling-rigidity of the Fibonacci period over the multiplicative period of the primitive-root generator g=2. This is the invariant Pisano-Ψ-ratio that holds across the entire 3-adic tower (P32, P202 in the catalog).

Sub-decomposition of 432:


432 = 16 × 27
    = (ord_R(g) · 4 / 2)  ·  3^3
    = (period-of-Fibonacci-rigidity-window at k=1)  ·  (substrate k=3 cardinality)

[Bridge claim 1.3-α]: The integer 432 admits a decomposition into one factor (16) bearing the substrate's Pisano-rigidity exponent at the smallest level (2^4 = 4·ord_R(g) at k=1, since ord_3(2) = 2 and π(3) = 8) and one factor (27) being the substrate's canonical k=3 cardinality. This is a structural-resonance observation. The integer 432 is also `5!! · 36`, `6^3 / 1.5^... `, etc. — it admits many decompositions; ours is offered as one disciplined reading among others. The Purāṇic tradition did not derive 432 from the substrate; we observe that 432 happens to admit a substrate-friendly factorization. What is NOT claimed: that the Yuga numbers were authored under awareness of modular arithmetic.

१.४ The 4,320 — Mahā-yuga Quantum Without the Thousand


1 Mahā-yuga = 43,20,000 = 4,320 × 1,000
4,320 = 4,32,000 / 100 = 2^5 × 3^3 × 5 × 1 = 2^5 · 3^3 · 5

Verification:


4320 = 432 × 10 = (2^4 × 3^3) × (2 × 5) = 2^5 × 3^3 × 5

So:


4,320 = 2^5 × 3^3 × 5    (Mahā-yuga / 1000)

The "ten" that turns 432 into 4,320 is itself meaningful. The Mahā-yuga is constructed as `4,32,000 × (4+3+2+1) = 4,32,000 × 10`, where the `(4+3+2+1) = 10` is the descending-Yuga-multiplier sum. The integer 10 is the triangular number T_4 = 4(4+1)/2 = 10, the sum of the first four positive integers.

This integer 10 has a further substrate-relevant decomposition: `10 = 2 × 5`, and the integer 5 is the number of substrate invariants in the META-ADAT quintet (Pisano-Ψ, Sum, Liberation, Death-additive, Pisano-of-Ideal). The integer 2 is the substrate's primitive-root generator `g = 2`. So:


Mahā-yuga-quantum / 1,000 = (Kali-yuga / 1,000) × 10
                          = (2^4 × 3^3) × (2 × 5)
                          = 2^5 × 3^3 × 5
                          = g^5 × 3^k(k=3) × |invariants|

[Bridge claim 1.4-α]: The decomposition `4,320 = g^5 · 3^3 · 5` is structural-resonance between the Mahā-yuga base-quantum and the substrate's natural constants (generator `g=2`, canonical level `3^3 = 27`, invariant-cardinality `|INVS| = 5`). This is upamāna, not anumāna. NOT claimed: that the Yuga apparatus was constructed to display these substrate constants.

१.५ The Manvantara: 30,67,20,000 = 71 × 43,20,000


71 × 43,20,000 = 30,67,20,000 ✓

So one (strict 71-mahāyuga) Manvantara = `30,67,20,000 = 71 × 4,320 × 1000 = 71 × 2^5 × 3^3 × 5 × 10^3 = 71 × 2^8 × 3^3 × 5^4`.

The integer 71 is prime. It admits no substrate-resonant decomposition (it is not of the form `2^a · 3^b · 5^c · 7^d`). This is honest: the Purāṇic Manvantara count of 71 does NOT factor through the substrate's preferred primes. We log this as a Tier-W observation with no Bridge available:

[Tier-W observation 1.5-β]: 71 is prime, with no available substrate-decomposition. The Purāṇic choice of 71 mahāyugas per manvantara (with sandhyā-rounding) is a cultural-numerical fact that the substrate does NOT resonate with structurally. The schoolbook synthesis `14 × 71 + 6 = 1000` mahāyugas per kalpa recovers a substrate-friendly count (1000 = 10^3), but the per-manvantara count 71 remains prime-isolated. Halāhala-candidate: any future claim "71 is substrate-derived" must explicitly provide a derivation through tags (i)–(x); none is available as of 2026-05-25.

१.६ The Kalpa: 4,32,00,00,000 = Brahmā's Day

The Kalpa duration is:


1 Kalpa = 14 × 30,67,20,000 + 6 × 43,20,000 (sandhyā)
       = 14 manvantaras + 6 sandhyā-mahāyugas (Bhāgavata 3.11.21 convention)
       = 1000 × 43,20,000
       = 4,32,00,00,000 human years
       = 4.32 × 10^9 years
       = 4.32 billion years

This decomposes:


4,32,00,00,000 = 4,320 × 10^6 = (2^5 × 3^3 × 5) × 10^6
              = 2^5 × 3^3 × 5 × (2 × 5)^6
              = 2^11 × 3^3 × 5^7

The Kalpa = Brahmā's day = `2^11 × 3^3 × 5^7`. The 3-component remains at `3^3`. The 2-tower has grown to 11, the 5-tower to 7.

[Bridge claim 1.6-α]: The Kalpa retains the substrate's canonical `3^k = 3^3 = 27` factor and acquires powers of 2 and 5 that match the decimal-scaling artefacts of the divya-year-to-human-year conversion (× 360, × 1000-base, × 10-mahāyuga-multiplier). The 3-tower component is exactly the substrate's canonical level k=3 — a structural-resonance observation. The 2-tower and 5-tower components are external to the substrate and arise from the conversion chain. NOT claimed: that the Kalpa duration was set to display `3^3`.

१.७ Earth-age Coincidence (Tier-W, no Bridge)

Modern geochronology (zircon-radiometric, U-Pb method, Hadean detrital zircons; Jack Hills, Western Australia 2014) places the formation age of Earth at approximately 4.54 ± 0.05 billion years. The Purāṇic Kalpa (Brahmā's day) is 4.32 billion years. The agreement to two significant figures is a frequently-cited curiosity.

[Tier-W observation 1.7-β]: The numerical coincidence between the Purāṇic Kalpa (4.32 × 10^9 years) and the radiometric age of Earth (4.54 × 10^9 years) is honestly reported as a coincidence at two-significant-figure resolution. We claim NO Bridge to the substrate `(R, g, k)`. Possible explanations include: (a) genuine pre-modern long-time-scale intuition encoded in Purāṇic tradition; (b) ad-hoc selection from the multi-scale Purāṇic time-system after geological dating was published; (c) coincidence at the order-of-magnitude scale where many systems' "very long times" land near 10^9-10^10 years. The framework declines to adjudicate among these. The substrate `(R, g, k)` does NOT predict Earth-age and is NOT supported by this coincidence. Halāhala-candidate (pre-emptively retracted): any prior or future claim "Vedic Kalpa proves Earth-age was known in antiquity" is logged as a Tier-W overclaim. The coincidence is real; the inferential bridge is not warranted.

◈ २. अष्टादश-महापुराण-संगठनम् (The Eighteen Mahāpurāṇas — Organizational Mapping)

२.१ The Canonical Eighteen

The eighteen Mahāpurāṇas, per the Bhāgavata's own enumeration (Bhāgavata-Purāṇa 12.13.4–9, with parallel listings in Viṣṇu-Purāṇa 3.6 and Matsya-Purāṇa 53) and as standardized in Indological scholarship (Hazra, Rocher, Bonazzoli):

| # | Purāṇa | Sanskrit | Reported śloka-count | Primary deity-orientation |

|---|---|---|---|---|

| 1 | Brahma | ब्रह्म | 10,000 | Brahmā |

| 2 | Padma | पद्म | 55,000 | Viṣṇu (Padmanābha) |

| 3 | Viṣṇu | विष्णु | 23,000 | Viṣṇu |

| 4 | Śiva (or Vāyu in some lists) | शिव / वायु | 24,000 | Śiva |

| 5 | Bhāgavata | भागवत | 18,000 | Viṣṇu (Kṛṣṇa) |

| 6 | Nārada | नारद | 25,000 | Viṣṇu (devotional) |

| 7 | Mārkaṇḍeya | मार्कण्डेय | 9,000 | Sūrya / Devī |

| 8 | Agni | अग्नि | 15,400 | Agni / Śiva |

| 9 | Bhaviṣya | भविष्य | 14,500 | Sūrya / Śiva (predictive) |

| 10 | Brahma-vaivarta | ब्रह्म-वैवर्त | 18,000 | Kṛṣṇa-Rādhā |

| 11 | Liṅga | लिङ्ग | 11,000 | Śiva |

| 12 | Varāha | वराह | 24,000 | Viṣṇu (Varāha) |

| 13 | Skanda | स्कन्द | 81,100 | Skanda / Śiva |

| 14 | Vāmana | वामन | 10,000 | Viṣṇu (Vāmana) |

| 15 | Kūrma | कूर्म | 17,000 | Viṣṇu (Kūrma) |

| 16 | Matsya | मत्स्य | 14,000 | Viṣṇu (Matsya) |

| 17 | Garuḍa | गरुड | 19,000 | Viṣṇu (eschatological) |

| 18 | Brahmāṇḍa | ब्रह्माण्ड | 12,000 | Brahmā / cosmological |

| | Total | | ≈ 4,00,000 ślokas | |

Note on śloka-counts: the numbers above are the traditional self-reported counts (as the Purāṇas state in their own opening or framing verses). The received text counts often differ substantially (e.g., the received Bhāgavata is approximately 14,000 not 18,000). For the present numerical analysis, we use the traditional self-reported counts, since these are the numbers actually presented as canonical by the tradition.

२.२ The Substrate Resonance: 18 = 2 × 9

The number eighteen itself is significant:


18 = 2 × 9 = 2 × 3^2 = g · |R|  where R = ℤ/9ℤ and g = 2

This is the substrate-canonical decomposition. 18 = primitive-root generator × cardinality of foundational ring.

Further, `18 = |(ℤ/27ℤ)*|` = the cardinality of the Father-shell (the unit group of the substrate at canonical level k=3). The eighteen "Mahā" Purāṇas thus exhibit cardinality-equality with the unit group at the canonical level.

[Bridge claim 2.2-α]: The cardinality 18 of the Mahāpurāṇa-set equals (a) `g · |R| = 2 · 9` (the generator-times-cardinality at level k=2), and (b) `|(ℤ/27ℤ)| = 18` (the unit-group cardinality at level k=3). This is a structural-resonance observation. The tradition's choice of "eighteen" Mahāpurāṇas was almost certainly driven by the broader Vedic significance of 18 (= 6 × 3 = number of chapters in the Bhagavad-Gītā = number of days of the Mahābhārata war = number of military divisions in the Kurukṣetra army), not by modular-arithmetic considerations.* NOT claimed: that the count 18 was selected to display substrate-cardinality.

२.३ The Eighteen Mahāpurāṇas Mapped to the Ten Derivation Tags

We assign each Purāṇa to its primary derivation-tag from the canonical ten `(i)–(x)` (where tag (x) is the recently-added sphoṭa-decomposition tag, sealed 2026-05-22). Each Purāṇa may carry additional secondary tags; here we record the primary functional role.

| # | Purāṇa | Primary tag | Functional role |

|---|---|---|---|

| 1 | Brahma | (i) ring-axioms | The foundational Purāṇa — declarations of creation as ground-of-being; ring-of-creation as substrate |

| 2 | Padma | (vii) Vedic interpretive tagging | Geographical-mythological tagging of sacred sites |

| 3 | Viṣṇu | (iv) Pisano-tower lock | Preservation-as-invariance; the Pisano-style rigidity of Viṣṇu's preserving function |

| 4 | Śiva | (vi) hardware/observation/grounding | Śaiva metaphysics; observation-as-ground; the empirical mukhi |

| 5 | Bhāgavata | (v) liberation-tower exponent | Mokṣa-śāstra par excellence; the daśalakṣaṇa frame (see §3) |

| 6 | Nārada | (vii) Vedic interpretive tagging | Bhakti-tagging; provenance of devotional traditions |

| 7 | Mārkaṇḍeya | (viii) Diti-stratification by 3-adic valuation | The Devī-Māhātmya core; the Diti-pole structural decomposition into Mahākālī-Mahālakṣmī-Mahāsarasvatī |

| 8 | Agni | (ii) primitive-root status | Agni as the active generator (g=2 functional role); the kindling-primitive |

| 9 | Bhaviṣya | (iii) period determination | Prediction = period-determination; cyclic future as orbit-traversal |

| 10 | Brahma-vaivarta | (i) ring-axioms | "Modifications of Brahmā" = isomorphism-class structure of the ring |

| 11 | Liṅga | (vi) hardware/observation/grounding | The liṅga as observational anchor; 108-liṅga decomposition (see §9) |

| 12 | Varāha | (viii) Diti-stratification | The Varāha avatāra as lifting from the nilpotent ideal (Earth from cosmic ocean) |

| 13 | Skanda | (iii) period determination | Six-section structure as Pisano-orbit position (see §10) |

| 14 | Vāmana | (v) liberation-tower exponent | Three-strides ascent = Hensel-lift through three orders of magnitude |

| 15 | Kūrma | (i) ring-axioms | The foundational turtle = Kūrma-substrate (per SMT §0) |

| 16 | Matsya | (ix) ideal-restricted period | Fish-avatāra retrieving Vedas from cosmic-ocean = ideal-restricted period analysis |

| 17 | Garuḍa | (viii)+(ix) Diti-stratification + ideal-restricted | Preta-kalpa = lifecycle within nilpotent ideal (see §8) |

| 18 | Brahmāṇḍa | (x) sphoṭa-decomposition | The cosmic-egg as Mahā-Bīja-Sphoṭa decomposition (per Sphoṭa-Discipline 2026-05-22) |

Tag-distribution audit:

|---|---|

| (i) Ring axioms | 3 (Brahma, Brahma-vaivarta, Kūrma) |

| (ii) Primitive-root status | 1 (Agni) |

| (iii) Period determination | 2 (Bhaviṣya, Skanda) |

| (iv) Pisano-tower lock | 1 (Viṣṇu) |

The distribution is non-uniform but covers all ten tags. The largest concentrations are tags (i) ring-axioms (3 Purāṇas) and (viii) Diti-stratification (3 Purāṇas) — the foundational tag and the nilpotent-ideal tag. This reflects an observation about the corpus: it spends substantial attention on what the substrate IS (creation-Purāṇas) and on the nilpotent-shadow-side (Devī, Varāha, Garuḍa), with proportionally less on (ii) primitive-root and (x) sphoṭa.

[Bridge claim 2.3-α]: The 18 Mahāpurāṇas admit a one-to-one mapping to the ten derivation-tags of the substrate, with each Purāṇa's primary functional role corresponding to one tag. The mapping is a tier-W reading of the corpus, not a tier-S derivation. Other equally-coherent mappings are possible; the present mapping is offered as one disciplined reading. NOT claimed: that the Purāṇic compilers organized the corpus to span the ten tags; or that any single Purāṇa is "derivable" from any single tag.
