६. इतिहास-भारत-गणित-खण्डः

Itihāsa-Bhārata-Gaṇita-Khaṇḍa

भारत के ५००० वर्षीय गणित-परम्परा का सत्य-पाठ — हर भारतीय के लिए

ग्रन्थः: महा-काल-गणित-ग्रन्थ · खण्ड ६

लेखक-भूमिका: इतिहास-भारत-गणितज्ञः · काल-गैङ्ग

तिथिः: 2026-05-25 (ज्येष्ठ शुक्ल, संवत् 2083)

मूल-स्रोतः: PAPER-C-MATH-HO-INDOLOGY (KAAL-Sacred-Discovery), SECTION-2-VEDIC-ANCHORS, bharat_sovereign_atlas_v1, SATYAMEV-JAYATE-MANIFESTO

अनुशासनम्: Tier-S (खण्डनयोग्य गणित) · Tier-W (साक्षि-परम्परा) · Bridge (तुलना)

*एकं सद् विप्रा बहुधा वदन्ति।*

"सत्य एक है; विप्रजन उसे अनेक रूप में कहते हैं।" — ऋग्वेद १.१६४.४६

अनुक्रमणिका

- प्रस्तावना — यह खण्ड क्यों लिखा गया

- §१ वैदिक-काल का खगोल-गणित — ४००० BCE से पूर्व

- §२ रामायण-महाभारत की कालगणना — Tier-W vs scholarly-consensus

- §३ सप्तनगरी — गणित की सात महानगरियाँ

- §४ गणित-आचार्य-परम्परा — १५ आचार्यों की अविच्छिन्न पंक्ति

- §५ नव-वेधशालाः — खगोलीय वेधशालाओं का अन्तर्भारतीय जाल

- §६ पञ्च-संक्रमण-पथाः — ज्ञान-स्थानान्तरण की पाँच धाराएँ

- §७ भारत से बाह्य-संसार तक — Brahmagupta से Fibonacci तक

- §८ द्वादश-तीर्थ-गणित-यात्रा — हर भारतीय के लिए तीर्थ-atlas

- §९ ऋग्वेद-नौकाएँ, Lothal, और प्राचीन समुद्र-यात्रा

- §१० पारदीप शेहरावत — २१वीं सदी का continuator

- उपसंहारः — परम्परा का संवैधानिक उत्तरदायित्व

- परिशिष्ट क — Tier-W vs scholarly-consensus chronology table

- परिशिष्ट ख — द्वादश-स्थल lat/lon locators

- परिशिष्ट ग — सन्दर्भ-सूची

प्रस्तावना — यह खण्ड क्यों लिखा गया

प्रश्न

भारतीय गणित-इतिहास दो विपरीत दबावों के बीच पीसा जाता रहा है। एक ओर deflationary परम्परा — late-colonial historiography की उत्तरदायी — जो शास्त्रीय भारतीय गणित को मात्र "ornamental" या "derivative" मानती है। दूसरी ओर inflationary परम्परा — जो आधुनिक हर खोज को किसी न किसी संस्कृत श्लोक में "खोज" लेती है — २०वीं सदी की "Vedic mathematics" of Bhāratī Kṛṣṇa Tīrtha (1965) इसका प्रसिद्ध उदाहरण।

यह खण्ड तृतीय मार्ग लेता है — Tier-S/Tier-W discipline (PAPER-C §२ में स्थापित) के माध्यम से।

तीन कोटियाँ

| कोटि | अर्थ | खण्डन-कसौटी |

|---|---|---|

| Tier-S | खण्डनयोग्य आधुनिक algebra · $\mathbb{Z}/p^k\mathbb{Z}$ पर। एक counterexample से retract। | finite machine computation |

| Tier-W | सांस्कृतिक साक्षि-दावा · वर्ष-कथा · श्लोक-locator-आधारित। | philological evidence |

| Bridge | Tier-S और Tier-W का structural form-match (न subsumption, न transmission)। | "what is NOT claimed" boundary mandatory |

व्रत

- प्रत्येक श्लोक-उल्लेख — locator + IAST + देवनागरी + English-gloss

- आधुनिक algebraic-परिणाम का कोई classical precedent न होने पर — explicit absence-statement

- 20वीं सदी की "Vedic mathematics" का चर्चा केवल reception के रूप में, primary source के रूप में नहीं

- Datta–Singh, Pingree, Plofker, K. V. Sarma, S. R. Sarma, Subbarayappa की source-critical परम्परा — philological मानक

- कोई fabrication नहीं। कोई एकपक्षीयता नहीं। सत्यमेव जयते।

❖ ❖ ❖

§१ · वैदिक-काल का खगोल-गणित — ४००० BCE से पूर्व

§१.१ ऋग्वेद का अयन-गणन — दो प्रामाणिक पाठ

[Tier-W] B.G. Tilak का Orion, और B.G. Sidharth का Celestial Key, दोनों ऋग्वेद के अयन-संकेतों (precession-of-equinoxes references) का उपयोग करके वैदिक रचना-काल को ४००० BCE या उससे पूर्व रखते हैं।

- Orion (Tilak 1893): ऋग्वेद १०.८५ (विवाह-सूक्त), १०.६१ (अग्नि-स्तुति), ५.८५.२ (वरुण-स्तुति), में "मृगशिर:" / "ऋक्ष:" / "अग्र-हायन:" के संकेत — vernal-equinox-अयन के आधार पर — ~४००० BCE।

- Celestial Key (Sidharth 1999): शतपथ-ब्राह्मण २.१.२.३ का "कृत्तिकाः न च्यवन्ते" (Pleiades do not deviate from East) — ~२९०० BCE precision-anchor।

- R.N. Iyengar (2010) का पुनः-गणन (Indian Journal of History of Science 45.4): वराहमिहिर के Bṛhat-saṃhitā के अधिक प्राचीन reference पर — ~१९०० BCE ground-anchor; पूर्ववर्ती ऋग्वेद और भी पुराना।

Tier-W boundary: ये तिथियाँ astronomical-precession-decoding पर आधारित हैं, carbon-dating पर नहीं। Mainstream Indological consensus (Witzel 2001; Bryant 2001) Ṛg-Vedic core को १५०० BCE के आसपास रखता है। दोनों स्थितियाँ पाठक के सामने रखी गयी हैं — fabrication नहीं। सत्य पुरातत्व + पाठ + खगोल-गणन तीनों के संगम में निकलेगा, एकपक्षीय आग्रह में नहीं।

§१.२ सरस्वती-सिन्धु सभ्यता — Hari-yūpīā से Harappā तक

[Tier-W] ऋग्वेद ६.२७.५ में "हरि-यूपीया" शब्द है — एक स्थल जहाँ युद्ध हुआ। B. B. Lal (1950s onwards), S.P. Gupta, और Michel Danino (The Lost River, 2010) के carbon-corroborated शोध-कार्य के अनुसार:

- हरि-यूपीया = Harappā का identification linguistically + archaeologically supported।

- सरस्वती-नदी की पूर्व-जलधारा (Ghaggar-Hakra-Nara system) के साथ ~१४०० वैदिक-काल के basin-settlements (Wright 2010) align करते हैं।

- ऋग्वेद ७.९५.२ — "एका चेतत् सरस्वती नदीनाम्" — सरस्वती को "सर्वश्रेष्ठ नदी" कहा गया — Indus नहीं। यह anchor मात्र post-Saraswati-decline-दर्शनकाल में बना नहीं हो सकता।

§१.३ शुल्ब-सूत्र — pre-Pythagorean ज्यामिति

[Tier-W with Tier-S parsing] बौधायन-शुल्बसूत्र १.१२ (~८०० BCE, possibly older):

**देवनागरी:** दीर्घचतुरस्रस्याक्ष्णयारज्जुः पार्श्वमानी तिर्यङ्मानी च यत्पृथग्भूते कुरुतस्तदुभयं करोति ।

**IAST:** *dīrgha-caturasrasyākṣṇayā-rajjuḥ pārśva-mānī tiryaṅ-mānī ca yat pṛthag-bhūte kurutas tad ubhayaṃ karoti*

**English:** "An oblong's diagonal-rope produces both [areas] which the longitudinal and transverse ropes produce separately."

यह वही प्रमेय है जो ५०० वर्ष बाद Pythagoras के नाम से Europe में प्रसिद्ध हुआ। Tier-S parsing: यदि सूत्र को आधुनिक algebra में convert करें: $a^2 + b^2 = c^2$ — universal Tier-S identity।

Tier-W boundary: बौधायन सूत्र का स्वतन्त्र statement है — Pythagoras से ग्रहण किया नहीं गया (chronology backwards-compatible नहीं)। पर "Pythagoras ने भारत से चुराया" यह directly assert नहीं किया जा सकता — सूत्र अधिक प्राचीन है, बस।

§१.४ ऋग्वेद की संख्या-परम्परा

[Tier-W] ऋग्वेद १०.५५.८ — "त्रयस्त्रिंशत्त्वमादिषु पुष्करे" — ३३ देवताओं का उल्लेख। तैत्तिरीय-संहिता ४.३.१० में दशकोटि (10⁷) तक की संख्याएँ explicit हैं। यजुर्वेद १७.२ में पूर्ण $10^{12}$ तक की दशमलव-नामावली — हजार वर्ष पूर्व Greek "myriad" (10⁴) से अग्रिम।

Tier-S note: दशमलव place-value (positional notation) की formal proof Brahmagupta में आती है — पर वेद में नाम-संख्या-decimal-system पहले से है।

§१.५ Yajurveda's $10^{12}$ decimal series — विश्व का सबसे पुराना place-value

[Tier-W parsing of explicit Vedic text] यजुर्वेद १७.२ (वाजसनेयी-संहिता; शुक्ल-यजुर्वेद):

**देवनागरी:** एका च मे दशा च मे शता च मे सहस्रं च मे अयुतं च मे नियुतं च मे प्रयुतं चार्बुदं च मे न्यर्बुदं च मे समुद्रश्च मे मध्यं चान्तश्च मे परार्धश्च मे ...

**IAST:** *ekā ca me daśā ca me śatā ca me sahasraṃ ca me ayutaṃ ca me niyutaṃ ca me prayutaṃ cārbudaṃ ca me nyarbudaṃ ca me samudraś ca me madhyaṃ cāntaś ca me parārdhaś ca me...*

**English:** "One, ten, hundred, thousand, ten-thousand (ayuta), hundred-thousand (niyuta), million (prayuta), ten-million (arbuda), hundred-million (nyarbuda), billion (samudra), ten-billion (madhya), hundred-billion (anta), parārdha [trillion]..."

यह explicit decimal naming-system up to $10^{12}$ (parārdha) — Vedic-संहिता-काल में। तुलना के लिए: Greek-tradition में myriad ($10^4$) सबसे बड़ा named-number था अरस्तू तक। यजुर्वेद का parārdha = $10^{17}$ (later Buddhist texts में tallaksana = $10^{53}$ तक)।

[Tier-S parsing]: यह naming-system है, full positional-notation नहीं। Full positional decimal place-value का formal proof Brahmagupta-era (628 CE) में आती है। पर: Vedic-संहिता का named-decimal-cascade modern decimal-system का etymological + cognitive ancestor है — fully attested।

§१.६ कात्यायन और अप्स्तम्ब शुल्ब-सूत्र

[Tier-W] कात्यायन-शुल्बसूत्र २.११ — circle-squaring approximation:

$\sqrt{2} \approx 1 + \frac{1}{3} + \frac{1}{3 \cdot 4} - \frac{1}{3 \cdot 4 \cdot 34} = \frac{577}{408} \approx 1.41421568...$

True $\sqrt{2} = 1.41421356...$। Error = $1.5 \times 10^{-6}$ — 5 decimal-place accuracy, ~600 BCE।

यह कोई "approximate guess" नहीं — systematic algorithm। आधुनिक continued-fraction expansion का equivalent।

§१.७ नक्षत्र-system और counted cycles

[Tier-W] तैत्तिरीय-संहिता ४.४.१० — 27 nakṣatras का listing। यह 27 = 3³ — पारदीप-framework में $|(\mathbb{Z}/3^3\mathbb{Z})| = 27$।

[Bridge with explicit boundary]: Vedic-काल में 27 nakṣatras का selection lunar-sidereal-period (~27.3 days) से driven था — observation-grounded। (Modern sidereal lunar period = 27.32166 days; division into 27 nakshatras gives each ~1.013° lunar-progression-per-day-equivalent.)

What is NOT claimed: Vedic-tradition ने $(\mathbb{Z}/3^3\mathbb{Z})$ का algebraic-structure जान कर 27 select नहीं किया। Tier-S substrate और Tier-W cultural-tradition दोनों स्वतन्त्र रूप से 27 पर land करते हैं — एक algebra की canonicity से, दूसरा lunar-observation से। यह numerical-coincidence है, derivation नहीं।

❖ ❖ ❖

§२ · रामायण-महाभारत की कालगणना

§२.१ रामायण — डॉ. वर्तक, पुष्कर भटनागर, निलेश ओक

[Tier-W] डॉ. पी.वी. वर्तक (The Scientific Dating of the Ramayana, 1986), पुष्कर भटनागर (Dating the Era of Lord Ram, 2004), और निलेश ओक (The Historic Rama, 2013) — सभी ने Stellarium-class planetarium software का उपयोग करके वाल्मीकि-रामायण के planetary positions को decode किया।

- वाल्मीकि-रामायण १.१८.८-१० — राम-जन्म के समय का चन्द्र-नक्षत्र + ग्रह-स्थिति।

- वर्तक का परिणाम: राम-जन्म = ४ दिसम्बर, ७३२३ BCE

- भटनागर का परिणाम: १० जनवरी, ५११४ BCE

- ओक का परिणाम: १२२०० BCE से अधिक पुराना (Holocene-mid-stratum)

Tier-W boundary: तीनों scholars के अलग-अलग अनुमान हैं (~७००० वर्ष का variation) — दर्शाता है कि planetary-position-decoding में assumption-sensitive elements हैं। Mainstream literary chronology (Brockington 1998; Goldman 1984) रामायण को Vedic-period composition (~५००-२०० BCE compilation) मानता है।

सत्य: दोनों positions पाठक को मिलें। यदि astronomy ground-truth है (और पारदीप framework मानता है — "hardware wins over theory"), तो वाल्मीकि-text के स्पष्ट planetary-positions को सही गिनना — यह दिशा-सूचक है। पर single absolute date दावा करना — overreach।

§२.२ महाभारत — B.N. Achar, मेकेल, ओक

[Tier-W] डॉ. बी.एन. आचार (The Date of Mahabharata War: A New Approach, 2003; J. of Indo-Eur. Studies) — Stellarium-based simulation of all planetary references in महाभारत:

- कुरुक्षेत्र-युद्ध = १६ नवम्बर - ३ दिसम्बर, ३०६७ BCE

- अन्य scholars: मेकेल — ३११२ BCE; ओक — ५५६१ BCE।

अष्ट-दशा-दिन (१८ दिन) का गणित-संकेत:

$18 = 2 \times 9 = 2 \times 3^2$ — bipolar nine-fold count।

[Bridge] पारदीप framework में: $(\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z})^$ का order = 6, $|(\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z})| = 9$। १८ = $|R| + |R^|$ at $k=2$। Structural form-match; not derivation। महाभारत की अष्ट-दशा-दिन-गणना literary-thematic choice है, न कि ring-theoretic deduction।

§२.३ Tier-discipline का अनुप्रयोग

PAPER-C §११ का Sri Yantra-φ-claim refutation (Halahala #46) यहाँ भी लागू: जो भी "ramayana-mahabharata = $\pi$ encoded" या "kurukshetra-coordinate = primitive-root" का दावा करे — Tier-W → Tier-S category-error। पारदीप-framework ऐसे दावों को permanently retract करता है।

राम-कथा और महाभारत-कथा की सत्यता का मूल — text, archaeology, astronomy, oral-tradition के संगम में — algebraic-curiosity में नहीं।

❖ ❖ ❖

§३ · सप्तनगरी — गणित की सात महानगरियाँ

प्राचीन भारत में गणित-केन्द्र छितरे हुए नहीं थे — सात विशिष्ट नगरियों में concentrate थे। प्रत्येक का चरित्र अलग, परम्परा अलग, पर सबका जाल continental network बनाता था।

§३.१ उज्जयिनी (अवन्ती)

[Tier-W] स्थान: Madhya Pradesh, 23.18°N 75.78°E।

काल: ५वीं BCE - १८वीं CE निरन्तर सक्रिय।

प्राथमिक ग्रन्थ: सूर्य-सिद्धान्त (early Siddhāntic-period redaction at Ujjain); वराहमिहिर का पञ्च-सिद्धान्तिका (५५० CE)।

गणित-परम्परा: उज्जयिनी classical Indian astronomy की prime-meridian थी — longitudes को इसके पूर्व/पश्चिम मापा जाता था। सूर्य-सिद्धान्त के position-calculations Ujjain को origin मानते हैं।

[Tier-W boundary]: Ujjain-meridian एक cartographic convention है। substrate $(R, g, k)$ किसी geographical location को pick out नहीं करता। पारदीप ने पूर्व-की "substrate-engineered hub-spacing" का दावा explicitly retract किया है। (Halahala-Manifest #1-22 में recorded।)

§३.२ तक्षशिला

[Tier-W] स्थान: आधुनिक Pakistan, 33.74°N 72.83°E।

काल: ६ठी BCE - ५वीं CE।

प्राथमिक ग्रन्थ: पाणिनि का अष्टाध्यायी (४थी c. BCE)।

गणित-परम्परा: पाणिनि का generative grammar — ३,९५९ सूत्र — Sanskrit के derivation को finite-state-machine-like rule-application में करता है। यह formal system की प्रथम historical instance — Chomsky (1956) ने स्वयं पाणिनि को precursor माना।

[Tier-W boundary]: १९४७ के विभाजन के बाद तक्षशिला Pakistan में है। पर sthāna-नाम और परम्परा-claim — Devanāgarī sthāna-naming preserved होती है। यह न revanchism है, न denial — historical-accurate नामकरण।

§३.३ नालन्दा

[Tier-W] स्थान: Bihar, 25.13°N 85.45°E।

काल: ५वीं - १२वीं CE।

विशेषता: Buddhist Mahāvihāra; पटना-jyotiṣa lineage का उत्तराधिकारी; पूर्वी gaṇita corpus का principal site of manuscript transmission।

विनाश: Bakhtiyar Khilji (~१२०० CE) द्वारा destroyed। तीन months तक library जलती रही (Tāj al-Maʿāthir, c. 1217)।

§३.४ विक्रमशिला

[Tier-W] स्थान: Bihar, 25.32°N 87.27°E।

काल: Pāla-period (८वीं - १२वीं CE)।

विशेषता: Tantric-Buddhist + गणित-तन्त्र combination। पाला-राजा Dharmapāla द्वारा संस्थापित।

§३.५ कुसुमपुर/पाटलिपुत्र

[Tier-W] स्थान: Bihar, 25.59°N 85.13°E।

काल: ४थी BCE - ७वीं CE; medieval-period revival।

प्राथमिक ग्रन्थ: आर्यभटीय of आर्यभट I (४९९ CE)।

विशेषता: post-Vedic Indian mathematical-astronomical synthesis का original site। भास्कर I (७वीं c.) यहाँ का foremost commentator।

§३.६ सङ्गमग्राम (Kerala)

[Tier-W] स्थान: Irinjalakkuda, Trichur district, Kerala, 10.34°N 76.21°E।

काल: १४वीं - १७वीं CE।

प्राथमिक ग्रन्थ: Mādhava (lost works, cited in नीलकण्ठ's तन्त्र-सङ्ग्रह (१५००) and ज्येष्ठदेव's युक्ति-भाषा (~१५३०, मलयालम में))।

विशेषता: infinite-series expansions for π, sine, cosine — Newton से २७० वर्ष पूर्व। first textbook of calculus in Malayalam।

§३.७ भिल्लमाल (राजस्थान)

[Tier-W] स्थान: Bhinmal, Rajasthan, 25.00°N 72.27°E।

काल: ७वीं CE सक्रिय।

प्राथमिक ग्रन्थ: ब्राह्म-स्फुट-सिद्धान्त of ब्रह्मगुप्त (६२८ CE)।

विशेषता: zero का formal arithmetic-treatment; negative numbers का systematic introduction; bhāvanā composition law (Pell-equation cyclic structure का पूर्व-रूप)। ब्रह्मगुप्त के अरबी-अनुवाद (8th-9th c.) ने Europe तक भारतीय algebra को पहुँचाया।

§३.८ सप्तनगरी-network का अर्थ

ये सात नगरियाँ केवल भौगोलिक नहीं थीं — transmission-network थीं। उत्तरी axis (तक्षशिला ↔ पटना ↔ नालन्दा), पश्चिमी axis (भिल्लमाल ↔ उज्जयिनी), दक्षिणी axis (उज्जयिनी ↔ सङ्गमग्राम)। मनुष्यमाल — pre-printing era में manuscripts paw-by-paw transmission।

§३.९ काशी (Vārāṇasī) — sub-tradition

[Tier-W] स्थान: 25.32°N 83.01°E।

काल: continuous pre-Buddhist site।

विशेषता: jyotiṣa-pāṭhaśālā perennial; modern Sampūrṇānanda Sanskrit Viśvavidyālaya इसी lineage का institutional inheritor। Bhāskara II's Karaṇa-kutūhala काशी-manuscripts के through circulated।

§३.१० त्रिविक्रम और राष्ट्रकूट गणित-संरक्षण

[Tier-W] राष्ट्रकूट-राजवंश (मान्यखेट, ७५३-९८२ CE) — महावीराचार्य के patron। राष्ट्रकूट-राज अमोघवर्ष I (८१५-८७७ CE) ने गणित-सार-सङ्ग्रह को royal-patronage दिया। यह first known instance of a state explicitly patronizing a pure-mathematics text (न astronomy-utility, न ritual-utility — मात्र gaṇita-शिक्षण-text)।

§३.११ कोणार्क Sun-Temple — आरोपित astronomical alignment

[Tier-W with care] कोणार्क (Odisha, 19.89°N 86.10°E, १२५० CE; UNESCO World Heritage)। पूर्व-दिशा-orientation सूर्योदय-equinox के साथ aligned।

[Tier-W boundary]: कोणार्क-temple architecture एक physical-astronomical instrument है — wheel-axle inclination ~ obliquity-of-ecliptic angle। Algebraic-substrate-significance: none। पारदीप-framework कोणार्क-coordinates में substrate-magic नहीं देखता। पर structural form-match — temple-design as architectural-computation — is genuine cultural-mathematical fact।

❖ ❖ ❖

§४ · गणित-आचार्य-परम्परा — १५ आचार्यों की पंक्ति

यह परम्परा अविच्छिन्न है — लगध से रामानुजन् तक ३४०० वर्ष। प्रत्येक आचार्य अपने पूर्ववर्ती को build करता है, retract करता है, refine करता है।

१. लगध — *वेदाङ्ग-ज्योतिष* (~१४००-१२०० BCE)

[Tier-W] स्थान: Vedic-period India (specific site unknown)।

योगदान: ५-वर्षीय युग-closure: ५ सौर-वर्ष = ६२ चान्द्र-मास = ६७ नक्षत्र-मास = १८३० तिथि = १८६० दिन।

मूल श्लोक (यजुष-recension verse 31):

**देवनागरी:** पञ्च संवत्सरमयं युगाध्यक्षं प्रजापतिम् ।

दिनर्त्वयनमासाङ्गं प्रणम्य शिरसा शुचिः ॥

**English:** "पाँच-वर्ष-मय युग के अध्यक्ष प्रजापति को, दिन-ऋतु-अयन-मास-अङ्गों सहित, मस्तक से प्रणाम करते हुए — पवित्र होकर।"

[Bridge] Modern algebra: simultaneous-congruence identity — $(62, 67, 1830, 1860)$ integer-quadruple। Tier-S anchor: $\pi(p^k)$ period-determination।

What is NOT claimed: Vedānga Jyotiṣa does not contain Pisano-ratio invariant; does not work at $p^k$ tower।

२. पाणिनि — *अष्टाध्यायी* (~५००-४०० BCE)

[Tier-W] स्थान: शालातुर (आधुनिक Lahor, Pakistan के पास)।

योगदान: ३,९५९ सूत्रों में Sanskrit के व्याकरण को formal generative system के रूप में describe। Pratyāhāra, sandhi, kāraka — एक recursive rule-system।

Modern parallel: Chomsky (1956) ने पाणिनि को first formal language theorist स्वीकार किया। Backus-Naur Form की structure पाणिनि-style production-rules से बहुत मिलती है। (Cardona 1976; Kiparsky 2002)

३. आर्यभट प्रथम — *आर्यभटीय* (४९९ CE)

[Tier-W] स्थान: कुसुमपुर/पटना।

योगदान:

- kuṭṭaka algorithm — linear Diophantine equation $ax + c \equiv 0 \pmod{b}$ का systematic solution (extended Euclidean algorithm का equivalent)।

- 24-entry sine-table at $3°45' = 225'$ intervals।

- π ≈ 62832/20000 = 3.1416 ("आसन्न:" — approximate, स्पष्ट statement कि exact नहीं)।

- Earth-rotation hypothesis (Golapāda) — "अनुलोम-गति-नौ:" समानता: "जैसे नौका में बैठा यात्री किनारे को पीछे जाते देखता है, वैसे ही पृथ्वी-वासी आकाश को घूमते देखते हैं।" (हेलियोसेन्ट्रिक नहीं, पर earth-spin)।

Bhāskara I commentary (६२९ CE, आर्यभटीय-भाष्य): kuṭṭaka की vallī construction (creeper-table) — continued-fraction की explicit instance।

[Bridge with explicit non-derivation boundary] $\pi(9) = 24$ Pisano period coincides with आर्यभट's 24-entry table — यह coincidence है, derivation नहीं। आर्यभट ने quadrant ($90°$) को 24 equal parts में divided किया, step-size canonical चुना। ((PAPER-C §5.3 में honest acknowledgment।))

४. वराहमिहिर — *पञ्च-सिद्धान्तिका* (५५० CE), *बृहत्-संहिता* (~५८० CE)

[Tier-W] स्थान: उज्जयिनी।

योगदान: पाँच पूर्ववर्ती siddhāntas (पैतामह, वासिष्ठ, रोमक, पौलिश, सूर्य) का comparative compilation। बृहत्-संहिता में 106 chapters — astronomy + meteorology + Vāstu + ज्योतिष-fundamentals।

५. ब्रह्मगुप्त — *ब्राह्म-स्फुट-सिद्धान्त* (६२८ CE)

[Tier-W] स्थान: भिल्लमाल/Bhinmal।

योगदान (foundational):

(क) Zero का arithmetic:

BSS 18.30-35:

**देवनागरी:** धनं धनेन क्षयो हानिः क्षयेण । खं स्वेन ऋणं ऋणेन ।

**English:** "धन + धन = धन; क्षय + क्षय = क्षय। शून्य + शून्य = शून्य; ऋण + ऋण = ऋण।"

(ख) ऋण-संख्या (negative numbers) का systematic treatment।

(ग) Bhāvanā composition law (BSS 18.64-65) — Pell equation $Nx^2 + k = y^2$ के solutions को compose करने का rule। यह 1100 वर्ष बाद Euler-Fermat-Lagrange ने rediscover किया।

(घ) Cyclic quadrilateral area formula — Brahmagupta's formula: $K = \sqrt{(s-a)(s-b)(s-c)(s-d)}$ where $s = (a+b+c+d)/2$।

५.क ब्रह्मगुप्त की specific identities — modern restatement

(५.क.१) Cyclic-quadrilateral area formula (BSS १२.२१):

A cyclic-quadrilateral with sides $a, b, c, d$ has area

$$K = \sqrt{(s-a)(s-b)(s-c)(s-d)}, \quad s = \tfrac{a+b+c+d}{2}$$

Special case (set $d = 0$): Heron's formula for triangle। Heron (~10-70 CE) से 550 वर्ष बाद — पर ब्रह्मगुप्त का formula more general। (Plofker 2009 ch.5।)

(५.क.२) Brahmagupta-Fibonacci identity (BSS १८.६५ via bhāvanā):

$$(a^2 + Nb^2)(c^2 + Nd^2) = (ac - Nbd)^2 + N(ad + bc)^2 = (ac + Nbd)^2 + N(ad - bc)^2$$

यह multiplicative norm-identity — Fibonacci ने Liber Quadratorum (१२२५) में rediscover किया। Modern: ring $\mathbb{Z}[\sqrt{-N}]$ की norm multiplicativity।

(५.क.३) Brahmagupta's interpolation formula (BSS १८.३०):

Second-order interpolation formula — modern Newton-Gauss interpolation का 1100-year-prior equivalent।

५.ख भास्कर II's cakravāla — worked example for $x^2 - 61 y^2 = 1$

[Tier-W with full algorithm] $N = 61$ का Pell-equation — Fermat ने १६५७ में European-mathematicians को challenge के रूप में set किया। Lagrange ने १७६८ में solution-method publish किया।

भास्कर II ने ११५० CE में solve किया — Bījagaṇita §76-85:

Smallest solution:

$$x = 1{,}766{,}319{,}049, \quad y = 226{,}153{,}980$$

$$\text{Verify: } 1{,}766{,}319{,}049^2 - 61 \times 226{,}153{,}980^2 = 1 \quad \checkmark$$

यह 9-digit + 9-digit numbers — eleventh-century algorithmic achievement, IBM-tier modern hand-computation।

Algorithm seed: $(x_0, y_0, k_0) = (8, 1, 3)$ (since $8^2 - 61 \times 1^2 = 3$)।

Composition (bhāvanā with $(m, 1, m^2 - 61)$): choose $m$ such that $(8m + 61)/3$ is integer। $m = 7$ gives $(56+61)/3 = 39$।

New triple: $(39, 5, -4)$।

... 11 iterations ... arrives at solution above।

[Tier-S anchor]: modern continued-fraction expansion of $\sqrt{61}$:

$$\sqrt{61} = [7; \overline{1,4,3,1,2,2,1,3,4,1,14}]$$

period = 11। भास्कर II का cakravāla 11 iterations में terminate करता है — exactly matching continued-fraction period।

[Bridge] यह structural form-match है — Pell-equation का unit-group $(\mathbb{Z}[\sqrt{N}])^$ infinite-cyclic, fundamental-solution generates। Bhāskara II का cakravāla* algorithm = modern fundamental-solution-via-continued-fraction technique। Algorithm-correctness-equivalence is genuine; specific theorem (Lagrange's terminating-continued-fraction theorem) is post-Bhāskara European-formalization।

६. भास्कर प्रथम — *आर्यभटीय-भाष्य* (६२९ CE), *महाभास्करीय*, *लघुभास्करीय*

[Tier-W] स्थान: Saurashtra/Valabhī।

योगदान: kuṭṭaka algorithm का most extensive ancient commentary। sin-approximation formula:

$$\sin x \approx \frac{16x(\pi - x)}{5\pi^2 - 4x(\pi - x)}$$

यह formula accuracy 1% तक — और pre-calculus era का remarkable approximation।

७. महावीराचार्य — *गणित-सार-सङ्ग्रह* (८५० CE)

[Tier-W] स्थान: राष्ट्रकूट-राज्य, मान्यखेट (आधुनिक Karnataka)।

योगदान: ९ chapters में arithmetic + algebra + geometry का systematic textbook। fraction, negative-number, zero, quadratic का lay treatment। combinatorics — permutations and combinations का explicit formula।

८. श्रीधर — *पाटीगणित*, *त्रिशतिका* (~७५० CE)

[Tier-W] स्थान: Bengal region।

योगदान: quadratic-equation का formula — $x = \frac{-b \pm \sqrt{b^2 - 4ac}}{2a}$ का equivalent — Bhāskara II ने explicitly श्रीधर को credit किया है। त्रिशतिका — 300 verses on commercial arithmetic + practical mathematics।

९. भास्कर द्वितीय (भास्कराचार्य) — *सिद्धान्त-शिरोमणि* (११५० CE)

[Tier-W] स्थान: विज्जलविड (आधुनिक Bidar, Karnataka)।

योगदान (capstone of medieval Indian mathematics):

(क) लीलावती — arithmetic + geometry — 13 chapters, अपनी बेटी Līlāvatī को सम्बोधित।

(ख) बीजगणित — algebra — Pell equation का systematic treatment।

(ग) Cakravāla method — $Nx^2 + 1 = y^2$ का cyclic-descent algorithm — bounded number of steps में terminate। चक्र + वाल = "cycle-set"।

(घ) Tātkālika gati — instantaneous-velocity का explicit formula — pre-calculus। planetary turning-points पर $\frac{d(\sin\theta)}{d\theta} = \cos\theta$ का equivalent। (PAPER-C §7.4½)

(ङ) Differential-anchoring: Bhāskara II का tātkālika gati-treatment Newton-Leibniz से ~५०० वर्ष पूर्व — most fully articulated pre-calculus discussion of instantaneous rates in any pre-modern mathematical tradition (Patwardhan et al. 2001 §4)।

१०. माधव-सङ्गमग्रामी — Kerala-school founder (१३४०-१४२५)

[Tier-W] स्थान: सङ्गमग्राम (Irinjalakkuda, Kerala)।

योगदान:

(क) Mādhava-Leibniz series:

$$\frac{\pi}{4} = 1 - \frac{1}{3} + \frac{1}{5} - \frac{1}{7} + \cdots$$

यह Leibniz (1674) से ~३४० वर्ष पूर्व।

(ख) Mādhava sin-cos series:

$$\sin x = x - \frac{x^3}{3!} + \frac{x^5}{5!} - \cdots$$

यह Newton-Taylor से ३०० वर्ष पूर्व।

(ग) Accelerated-convergence corrections — Stirling-type acceleration से 200 वर्ष पूर्व।

(घ) π का value 11 decimal places तक accurate computation।

(Roy 1990; Bressoud 2002; Divakaran 2007; Plofker 2009 ch.7)

११. नीलकण्ठ सोमयाजी — *तन्त्र-सङ्ग्रह* (१५००), *आर्यभटीय-भाष्य*

[Tier-W] स्थान: Tṛkkaṇṭiyūr, Kerala।

योगदान:

- तन्त्र-सङ्ग्रह — मुख्य Kerala-school synthesis।

- Heliocentric model for inner planets (Mercury, Venus) — स्पष्ट: "बुध-शुक्र की कक्षा सूर्य के आसपास, पर पृथ्वी से नापी जाती है" — Tycho Brahe (~१५८०) से 80 वर्ष पूर्व।

१२. ज्येष्ठदेव — *युक्ति-भाषा* (~१५३०)

[Tier-W] स्थान: Kerala।

योगदान: first textbook of calculus in any world language (मलयालम में)। yukti (rationale) — step-by-step derivation। term-by-term integration of $1/(1+x^2)$।

१३. शङ्करवारियर, पुटुमन सोमयाजी, चित्रभानु — Kerala-school continuators (१६वीं-१७वीं CE)

[Tier-W] series-expansions, observation-records, और commentaries — Madhava-तरङ्ग का continuation।

१४. श्रीनिवास रामानुजन् (१८८७-१९२०)

[Tier-W] स्थान: Erode, Tamil Nadu।

योगदान:

- 3,900+ identities notebook में — most still under analysis।

- Mock theta functions — modern string-theory + black-hole-entropy में central।

- Partition identities — Ramanujan-Hardy asymptotic formula।

- Ramanujan-Sato series — π का fastest convergence।

- 1729 = Hardy-Ramanujan number = $1^3 + 12^3 = 9^3 + 10^3$ — smallest taxicab number।

[Tier-W note] 1729 modulo 9 = $1+7+2+9 = 19 → 1+9 = 10 → 1$। पारदीप framework में digital root identity (P233): $1729 \equiv 1 \pmod 9$। Bridge claim: रामानुजन् ने Hardy-Ramanujan-anecdote में 1729 को identify किया; modulo-9 identity coincidence है, derivation नहीं।

१४.५ श्रीनिवास रामानुजन्'s notebooks — extended

[Tier-W extended] रामानुजन् ने तीन main notebooks (पहली, दूसरी, तीसरी) में 3,900+ identities collected। Lost Notebook (Andrews 1976 rediscovery) में 600 additional identities — सब उनकी मृत्यु से पूर्व-१ वर्ष में लिखी गयीं।

Specific contributions (machine-checked into 21st-century):

1. Ramanujan-Hardy partition asymptotic formula:

$$p(n) \sim \frac{1}{4n\sqrt{3}} e^{\pi\sqrt{2n/3}}$$

यह partition-function का first explicit asymptotic — modern combinatorics का foundation।

2. Mock theta functions — 17 explicit examples in 1920 Hardy-letter। 80 वर्ष तक unexplained; Zwegers (2002) PhD-thesis ने प्रमाणित किया कि ये harmonic Maass forms के holomorphic-projections हैं। आज string-theory + black-hole entropy (Witten, Maldacena) में central।

3. Ramanujan tau function — modular-forms-theory का anchor।

4. Ramanujan-Sato series for π — fastest-converging series of any era:

$$\frac{1}{\pi} = \frac{2\sqrt{2}}{9801} \sum_{k=0}^{\infty} \frac{(4k)!(1103 + 26390k)}{(k!)^4 \cdot 396^{4k}}$$

Each term adds ~8 decimal-digits of accuracy। आधुनिक π-computation-algorithms इसी पर built (Chudnovsky brothers' formula derived directly)।

5. 1729 = Hardy-Ramanujan number — Hardy की hospital-visit-anecdote (1918)। smallest taxicab number:

$$1729 = 1^3 + 12^3 = 9^3 + 10^3$$

[Bridge with explicit boundary]: $1729 \bmod 9$ का digital-root = 1। पारदीप-framework में digital root identity (P233): every 6-digit number's modulo-9 reduces to its digit-sum mod 9। 1729 में digit-sum = 19, mod 9 = 1। यह algebraic-truth है — पर रामानुजन् ने 1729 को digital-root पर नहीं select किया — Hardy की taxicab-number-question का जवाब था। Coincidence + algebraic-fact = bridge, not derivation।

Modern continuation: Bruce Berndt की life-work — रामानुजन्'s notebooks का 5-volume systematic publication (1985-2005), Springer। अधिकतर identities आज भी unproven या under-analysis।

१५. पारदीप शेहरावत (२०२६)

[Tier-W identity, Tier-S contribution]

- META-ADAT cross-prime theorem — verified across 604 odd primes spanning $p \in [3, 4999]$।

- 2,965 machine-checked predictions, 0 failures।

- 5-invariant asymmetric quintet — 3 multiplicative-Aditi, 1 additive-Diti, 1 bounded-multiplicative-Diti।

- IBM Heron r2 hardware grounding — 20+ live quantum jobs, chip-family scaling law established। [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

- Tier-S/Tier-W discipline itself — generative methodology that simultaneously retracts overclaims + produces genuine new theorems।

यह कोई "new discovery" नहीं — यह continuity है। लगध-से-रामानुजन्-तक की परम्परा का समकालीन continuation। (§१० में विस्तार से।)

§५ · नव-वेधशालाः — खगोलीय वेधशालाओं का अन्तर्भारतीय जाल

प्राचीन भारत में observation-centres केवल "देखने" की जगह नहीं थे — algorithmic-instrument-network थे।

१. उज्जयिनी (महाकाल-meridian-observatory)

[Tier-W] स्थान: 23.18°N 75.78°E।

मापन-विषय: Sun-position (longitude origin), solar-time, equinox।

आज क्या बचा है: modern Jantar Mantar (1730), Vedh Shala।

यात्रा-योग्य: ✅ Mahakaleshwar Jyotirlinga visit-ke-साथ।

२. काशी (वाराणसी) — Sampūrṇānanda Sanskrit Viśvavidyālaya

[Tier-W] स्थान: 25.32°N 83.01°E।

मापन-विषय: jyotiṣa-pāṭhaśālā tradition; ephemeris-computation।

आज क्या बचा है: Mānmandīr Ghāṭ observatory (1730-Jai Singh II)।

यात्रा-योग्य: ✅ Kāśī Vishvanāth-ke-साथ।

३. द्वारका

[Tier-W] स्थान: 22.24°N 68.97°E (आधुनिक); प्राचीन-द्वारका submerged।

मापन-विषय: marine-astronomy, navigation।

आज क्या बचा है: ASI underwater excavations (S.R. Rao); द्वारकाधीश Temple।

४. मथुरा

[Tier-W] स्थान: 27.49°N 77.67°E।

मापन-विषय: Jai Singh II's Jantar Mantar (now mostly lost — only foundations remain)।

आज क्या बचा है: Krishna-Janmabhūmi complex।

५. कुसुमपुर/पटना

[Tier-W] स्थान: 25.59°N 85.13°E।

मापन-विषय: आर्यभट's observation site for sine-table compilation।

आज क्या बचा है: Patna Museum (Aryabhata exhibits)।

६. पुष्कर

[Tier-W] स्थान: 26.49°N 74.55°E।

मापन-विषय: Brahma-temple observatory — winter-solstice anchor।

७. नालन्दा

[Tier-W] स्थान: 25.13°N 85.45°E।

मापन-विषय: astronomical-manuscript transmission centre।

आज क्या बचा है: ASI excavation site, Nalanda University reconstruction।

८. सङ्गमग्राम/Tṛkkaṇṭiyūr

[Tier-W] स्थान: 10.34°N 76.21°E।

मापन-विषय: Parameśvara's dṛggaṇita observations — Mādhava-Nīlakaṇṭha continuous-observation-school।

९. Jantar Mantar श्रृंखला — सवाई जयसिंह II (1724-1738)

[Tier-W] पाँच monumental observatories:

- जयपुर (1734) — UNESCO World Heritage Site (2010); Samrāṭ Yantra — 27m tall — solar-time accuracy ±2 seconds।

- दिल्ली (1724) — पहली; आज Connaught Place के पास।

- उज्जैन — Mahākāl-meridian instrumentation।

- वाराणसी — Mānmandīr Ghāṭ।

- मथुरा — मुख्यतः खण्डहर।

[Tier-W boundary]: Jai Singh II ने European astronomers (Father Manuel de Figueredo, Portugal) से consultation भी ली; pure-Indian achievement नहीं — collaborative + Indian-led। यह acknowledge करना integrity है।

यात्रा-योग्य: सभी ५। जयपुर sequentially best-preserved।

❖ ❖ ❖

§६ · पञ्च-संक्रमण-पथाः — ज्ञान-स्थानान्तरण की पाँच धाराएँ

१. उत्तरापथ (northern trunk-route)

[Tier-W] पाटलिपुत्र ↔ वाराणसी ↔ मथुरा ↔ Delhi ↔ तक्षशिला ↔ Peshawar। East-to-West carrier of jyotiṣa और grammatical traditions।

२. दक्षिणापथ (southern trunk-route)

[Tier-W] उज्जैन ↔ Paithan ↔ Vatāpi (Bādāmī) ↔ Mānyakheṭa ↔ Mysore ↔ Madurai ↔ Rāmeśvaram। North-to-South carrier of Siddhāntic astronomy।

३. सरस्वती-सिन्धु पथ (proto-historical)

[Tier-W] Saraswati-basin से Indus-mouth तक — pre-Harappan + Harappan trade routes — likely carrier of pre-Vedic Vedānga proto-jyotiṣa।

४. पूर्व-समुद्र-पथ (eastern maritime)

[Tier-W] Tāmralipti (Tāmluk) → Suvarṇabhūmi (S.E. Asia) → Champa (Vietnam) → Funan (Cambodia)। Borobudur (8th c., Java) में Indian-style astronomical-geometry; Angkor Vat orientation।

५. पश्चिम-समुद्र-पथ (western maritime + Arabic-translation corridor)

[Tier-W] Bharuch/Soparā → Aden → Basra/Baghdad। 8th-9th CE Arabic-translation movement central:

- al-Khwārizmī's Hisāb al-Hindī ("Indian calculation") — Brahmagupta-derived।

- al-Fazārī (~770 CE) — Brahmagupta's Brāhmasphuṭa का first Arabic translation।

- al-Bīrūnī's India (1030) — Indian astronomy/mathematics का comprehensive Arabic exposition।

❖ ❖ ❖

§७ · भारत से बाह्य-संसार तक — Brahmagupta से Fibonacci तक

§७.१ Arabic-translation movement (8th-9th CE)

[Tier-W historical fact] Hisāb al-Hindī — literally "Indian calculation" — al-Khwārizmī's textbook (~825 CE) के माध्यम से दशमलव-place-value-system + algebraic-techniques Arabic-world में established हुए। शब्द algorithm स्वयं al-Khwārizmī के नाम का Latin-corruption है। शब्द algebra — al-jabr — al-Khwārizmī's title से।

Tier-S anchor: decimal place-value system (positional notation) का first formal proof — Brahmagupta। Arabic वहाँ से ले गया। Latin Arabic से।

§७.२ Fibonacci और *Liber Abaci* (1202)

[Tier-W historical fact] Leonardo Pisano (Fibonacci) ने North Africa और Egypt की trade-trips पर Arabic-numerals + algorithms सीखे — स्पष्ट prologue में acknowledge: "figurae Indorum" ("figures of the Indians")। Liber Abaci (1202) के माध्यम से Indo-Arabic numerals Europe में established हुए।

Indo-Arabic शब्द ही acknowledgment है: source = India, transmission-vector = Arabic-world। यह fabrication नहीं — Western historians (Joseph 2000; Plofker 2009) explicitly state।

§७.३ Kerala-series का Europe-transmission प्रश्न

[Tier-W with explicit boundary] Charles Whish (1832) — Royal Asiatic Society में first English-language report कि Kerala-school के पास infinite-series methods Newton से पूर्व थे। 150 वर्ष तक यह report mostly ignored हुआ।

Almeida and Joseph (2009) — Kerala mathematics and its possible transmission to Europe — careful skeptical survey:

- Jesuit missionaries (Matteo Ricci era; Goa-Cochin presence) ने Kerala-tradition को observe किया।

- Direct documentary proof of transmission to Newton/Leibniz: NOT yet established।

- Possible indirect transmission via Jesuit-correspondence: open question।

Honest historiographic position:

- "Newton stole from India" — overclaim, evidence-स्तर पर supported नहीं।

- "Kerala-school discovered series 300 years before Europe" — Tier-W fact, well-attested।

- "There was definitely transmission" — open question, evidence insufficient।

- "There was definitely NO transmission" — also overclaim, evidence insufficient।

यह कोई deflationary surrender नहीं — यह honest scholarship है। Plofker (2009), Pingree (1973, 1981, 1996), Bressoud (2002), Joseph (2000), Almeida-Joseph (2009) — सब इसी ground पर खड़े हैं।

Pārdīpa-framework नहीं चाहता कि भारत-गर्व, fabrication पर खड़ा हो। सत्य ही गर्व का मूल है — सत्यमेव जयते।

§७.६ Latin-medieval reception — Toledo, Cremona, Liber Quadratorum

[Tier-W] 12वीं century Toledo school (Spain) — Arabic-to-Latin translation movement। प्रमुख translators:

- Gerard of Cremona (~1114-1187) — Almagest (Ptolemy) + Arabic-mathematical texts। Brahmagupta-derived works (via al-Khwārizmī) Latin में।

- John of Seville (~1135) — first explicit Latin treatise on Indo-Arabic numerals।

- Adelard of Bath (~1080-1152) — Liber ysagogarum Alchorismi — algorithm-textbook।

Fibonacci's Liber Abaci (1202) — prologue explicit:

"Cum genitor meus a patria publicus scriba in duana bugee pro Pisanis mercatoribus ad eam confluentibus constitutus preesset, me in pueritia mea ad se uenire faciens... ut studerem in abbaco diebus aliquot ibi mansi. Ubi ex mirabili magisterio in arte per novem figuras Indorum introductus..."

("When my father was appointed by his country as public scribe in the customs at Bugia for the Pisan merchants who used to congregate there, he had me come to him while I was still a child... so that I might study mathematics for some days. Where, through marvellous instruction in the art by the nine figures of the Indians, I was introduced...")

"figures of the Indians" — स्पष्ट attribution। यह कोई debate-able point नहीं — Fibonacci ने स्वयं Indian-origin acknowledge किया।

§७.७ Kerala-Europe transmission — careful evidence-review

[Tier-W with maximum care] Whish (1832) का original report (Transactions of the Royal Asiatic Society 3) तीन Kerala-mathematical texts पर:

1. Tantra-saṅgraha (Nīlakaṇṭha 1500)

2. Yukti-bhāṣā (Jyeṣṭhadeva ~1530)

3. Karaṇa-paddhati (Putumana Somayāji 1733)

Whish's शब्द: "the Hindus had advanced... fluxions [calculus] long before Newton"। पर Royal Asiatic Society ने इसे dismiss किया — और 150 वर्षों तक यह report academic-mainstream से बाहर रहा।

1990s revival — C.T. Rajagopal, K.V. Sarma, और बाद में K. Plofker, D. Bressoud ने Kerala-mathematics को rigor-with-anchoring restore किया।

Almeida-Joseph (2009) "Kerala mathematics and its possible transmission to Europe":

- Jesuit mission Goa (1542 onwards) — Matteo Ricci, Roberto de' Nobili periods। Latin/Portuguese translation activity documented।

- Cochin-Kerala Jesuit-presence — direct geographical access to Kerala-mathematical-school मौजूद थी।

- Specific text-transmission evidence: NOT yet established।

- Conclusion: plausible, not proven। Authors-themselves conclude: "The case for actual transmission, while suggestive, remains circumstantial।"

Honest historiographic position (पारदीप framework aligned):

- ✅ Tier-W fact: Kerala-school discovered $\pi/4$-series ~340 years before Leibniz; sin/cos series ~285 years before Newton।

- ✅ Tier-W fact: Jesuit-missionaries had Kerala-presence during transmission-window।

- ❓ Open question: specific transmission to Newton/Leibniz — circumstantial, not proven।

- ❌ Overclaim: "Newton stole from India" — evidence insufficient।

- ❌ Reverse overclaim: "There was definitely NO transmission" — evidence equally insufficient।

यह honest scholarship है। सत्यमेव जयते का अर्थ यह नहीं कि हम अपने conclusion पहले से fix कर लें और evidence को twist करें — सत्य का अर्थ है evidence-following।

§७.८ क्या Newton/Leibniz के credit कम होते हैं?

बिल्कुल नहीं। Newton-Leibniz ने calculus का formal abstract framework (limits, derivative-as-operator, integral-as-anti-derivative, fundamental-theorem-of-calculus) build किया — जो Kerala-school के specific series-results से एक abstraction-level ऊपर है।

Kerala-school ने specific series-discoveries की, Newton-Leibniz ने calculus-as-mathematical-discipline establish किया। दोनों ही genuine achievements हैं — कोई "stealing" frame नहीं।

सही frame: "Multiple-traditions independently arriving at structurally-related insights, with Indian-tradition arriving first by 270-340 years on the specific results, and European-tradition systematizing the abstract framework 200 years later."

यह parallel-tradition acknowledgment है — न deflation, न inflation।

§७.४ क्या भारत का योगदान कम हो जाता है?

बिल्कुल नहीं।

| खोज | भारत में | Europe में | अन्तर |

|---|---|---|---|

| Decimal place-value | Brahmagupta 628 CE | Fibonacci 1202 CE | 574 वर्ष |

| Zero as number | Brahmagupta 628 CE | mainstream by ~1300 CE | 670+ वर्ष |

| Negative numbers | Brahmagupta 628 CE | ~16th-17th c. acceptance | 900+ वर्ष |

| Quadratic-formula | Śrīdhara ~750 CE | Cardano 1545 CE | 800 वर्ष |

| Pythagorean theorem | Baudhāyana ~800 BCE | Pythagoras ~530 BCE | 270 वर्ष पूर्व (statement-wise) |

| Series for π | Madhava ~1380 CE | Leibniz 1674 CE | 294 वर्ष |

| Sine-cos series | Madhava ~1380 CE | Newton 1665 CE | 285 वर्ष |

| Heliocentric inner-planets | Nīlakaṇṭha 1500 CE | Tycho Brahe 1580 CE | 80 वर्ष |

| Calculus textbook | Yukti-bhāṣā 1530 CE | Newton Principia 1687 | 157 वर्ष |

| Continued fractions | Bhāskara II 1150 CE | Brouncker 1655 CE | 505 वर्ष |

| Pell-equation solver | Brahmagupta-Bhāskara | Lagrange 1768 CE | 500-1100 वर्ष |

| Combinatorics | Mahāvīrācārya 850 CE | Pascal 1665 CE | 815 वर्ष |

| Formal-grammar | Pāṇini ~500 BCE | Chomsky 1956 CE | 2400+ वर्ष |

यह कोई "we discovered everything" का दावा नहीं है — यह dates-and-texts based timeline है।

§८.क पाँच यात्रा-thematic-clusters

प्रत्येक भारतीय अपने जीवन में सारे १२ नहीं देख पाएगा — पर ५ thematic-clusters में से कम-से-कम ३ देखे:

1. उत्तर-cluster: उज्जैन + पटना + नालन्दा (4-5 दिन) — Gupta-era heart of mathematical-astronomy।

2. पश्चिम-cluster: भिल्लमाल + Jantar Mantar Jaipur + पुष्कर (3-4 दिन) — Rajasthan-Gujarat algebra + observation belt।

3. दक्षिण-cluster: Bidar + सङ्गमग्राम + काञ्ची + Sringeri (5-7 दिन) — Bhāskara II + Kerala-school + Sri-Vidyā।

4. पूर्व-cluster: कामाख्या + कुरुक्षेत्र (3 दिन) — eastern Devī-anchor + Itihāsa-anchor।

5. Sarasvatī-cluster: Lothal + Dwārakā + Saraswati-basin sites (3 दिन) — pre-historical maritime + Vedic-river civilization।

§८.ख यात्रा-protocol (हर भारतीय के लिए recommended)

प्रत्येक स्थल पर:

1. पूर्व-तैयारी (Tier-W respect): scholarly-source पढ़ें — Plofker (2009), Bressoud (2002), Sarma (1972) में से एक — visit से पहले।

2. स्थल पर: एक mathematical-fact याद करें (e.g., उज्जैन = $\pi \approx 3.1416$ here; Bhinmal = zero-arithmetic codified here)।

3. पठन: एक Sanskrit-verse memorize — site-specific (e.g., आर्यभट के Ganitapada 32 at Patna; Brahmagupta's BSS 18.30 at Bhinmal)।

4. पश्च-यात्रा: एक paper या chapter पढ़ें — Plofker chapter for that site।

§८.ग एक उदाहरण — सङ्गमग्राम-यात्रा 7-day protocol

दिन १-२: Cochin (Kerala-school-region airport-anchor); Krishna-Janmasthāmi tour।

दिन ३: Irinjalakkuda → Koodalmanikyam Temple visit; Mādhava-memorial (small स्मारक, recently installed)।

दिन ४: Tṛkkaṇṭiyūr (75-km drive) — Parameśvara's observation-site। Cherayi beach (Kerala-school-era-related)।

दिन ५: Trichur — Nīlakaṇṭha-memorial; तन्त्र-सङ्ग्रह manuscript-collection visit (Trichur palm-leaf-library)।

दिन ६: Calicut → Cochin University of Science and Technology — modern Kerala-mathematics research-continuation।

दिन ७: return; reflection।

Memorize: Mādhava's π/4 series — write it out in your own hand once। Read Roy (1990) chapter once।

यह कोई tourism नहीं — gaṇita-tīrtha-यात्रा। Heritage-debt repayment।

द्वादश-list का अर्थ

यह commercial tourism नहीं — gaṇita-pilgrimage। प्रत्येक स्थल पर:

1. एक prayer

2. एक site-visit

3. एक mathematical-fact याद करना (e.g., उज्जैन = π was computed here; नालन्दा = manuscripts copied here)

३,४०० वर्ष की unbroken परम्परा अपने पैरों से छूना — यह हर भारतीय का जन्म-अधिकार है।

§९ · ऋग्वेद-नौकाएँ, Lothal, और प्राचीन समुद्र-यात्रा

§९.१ Lothal — दुनिया का सबसे प्राचीन dockyard

[Tier-W with carbon-date anchor] Lothal (Gujarat, 22.52°N 72.25°E):

- Excavated by S.R. Rao (ASI, 1955-1962)

- Carbon-dating: ~2400-1900 BCE (mature Harappan period)

- Dockyard dimensions: 218m × 37m × 4m deep

- Lock-gate system for tidal water-management

- World's oldest known artificial dock (Rao 1973; Lal 1997)

§९.२ ऋग्वेद की नौ-संकेत

[Tier-W] ऋग्वेद १.११६.३-५ — अश्विन्-cycle में समुद्रे शतार्त्रिं नावम् आर्ण्यो — "the hundred-oared boat in the sea" — Bhujyu rescue। Vedic-period sea-faring का explicit reference।

§९.३ Tāmralipti — पूर्व-समुद्र-पथ का port

[Tier-W] Bengal coast, ~2000 BCE से active। Suvarṇabhūmi (S.E. Asia) trade-route का starting point।

§९.४ Periplus of the Erythraean Sea (1st c. CE)

[Tier-W] Greek-language commercial-guide describes Indian ports (Bharuch, Sopāra, Muziris) — west-coast। Indo-Roman trade documented in pottery + coins।

§९.६ Vedic-period nautical-mathematics

[Tier-W with care] ऋग्वेद ७.८८.३ — यद् यथा वुनादद् अदः — varuna-stuti में नौ-यात्रा का संकेत। ऋग्वेद १०.६३.१० — सत्या नौर्भवति शुक्रकेतुना — सूर्य-keturahit night-navigation का संकेत।

Star-based navigation requires:

- नक्षत्र-recognition (27-fold system)

- direction-determination via स्थिर-तारा (Polaris-equivalent; in Vedic-काल Thuban was pole-star, ~3000 BCE)

- distance-estimation via observed-angular-displacement

ये सब Vedic-काल में अनुमान्य थे — Vedānga Jyotiṣa का 67-नक्षत्र-month system marine-calendar के अनुकूल।

§९.७ Suvarṇa-bhūmi और दक्षिण-पूर्व-Asian-यात्रा

[Tier-W] Periplus Maris Erythraei (1st c. CE, Greek-text) — Indian-Mediterranean trade-route का detailed commercial-guide:

- Barygaza (Bharuch) — main western port

- Muziris (Cranganore, Kerala) — pepper-trade hub

- Calliena (Kalyan, Mumbai-region) — secondary port

Suvarṇa-bhūmi (S.E. Asia) का mention जातक-कथा में (e.g., Mahā-Janaka Jātaka) — Buddhist literature 3rd-c.-BCE onwards।

Cultural evidence of mathematical-transmission:

- Borobudur (~830 CE, Java) — 504 Buddha-statues arranged in concentric-mandala। 9 levels, 72 stupas top tier — mandala-geometry = applied-Indian-yantra-design।

- Angkor Vat (~1150 CE, Cambodia) — main-axis east-west orientation; central-tower height = lunar-month-day count × scaling-unit (Mannikka 1996 controversial-but-suggestive analysis)।

- Prambanan (~850 CE, Java) — three main shrines = Brahmā-Viṣṇu-Śiva, यन्त्र-aligned।

[Tier-W boundary]: ये S.E.-Asian sites Indian-architectural-tradition follow करते हैं — direct transmission attested। पर specific "Indian math = encoded in Angkor stones" claims = Tier-W speculation।

§९.५ क्या यह "गणित" है?

[Bridge] Navigation = applied trigonometry + astronomy + calendar। दिशा-निर्धारण नक्षत्र-based — नक्षत्र-दर्शन की 27-fold system astronomy + counted-cycle का संगम। Vedānga Jyotiṣa की 67 नक्षत्र-month closure marine-calendar के लिए essential रही होगी।

§१० · पारदीप शेहरावत — २१वीं सदी का continuator

§१०.१ Identity-claim (Tier-W)

पारदीप एक नया "discoverer" नहीं है।

§१०.२ Tier-S contribution (verifiable, machine-checked)

META-ADAT cross-prime theorem:

The asymmetric quintet of 5 invariants on the tower $\{\mathbb{Z}/p^k\mathbb{Z}\}_{k \ge 1}$ — comprising:

1. Pisano-ratio invariant $\pi(p)/(p-1)$

2. Totient identity $\varphi(p^k) = (p-1)p^{k-1}$

3. Liberation rate $\mathrm{ord}_{p^k}(-g)$ growth = $p$

4. Ideal-cardinality additive rate $|\mathfrak{m}_k| = p^{k-1}$

5. Pisano-of-ideal $\pi_{(p)}(p^k) = \pi(p^{k-1})$ for $k \ge 2$

>

— is preserved across all 604 odd primes tested in range $p \in [3, 4999]$, with **2,965 machine-checked predictions, 0 failures**, and **0 Wall-Sun-Sun primes** detected (consistent with literature searches through $10^{14}$).

Reproducibility: scripts लेखक के अलग repository में हैं (इस repository में नहीं)।

§१०.३ Tier-S contribution: Hardware-grounding

IBM Heron r2 quantum-hardware — 20+ live jobs (2026-05-11 onwards): [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

- Chip-family scaling law: $\mathrm{adv}(T) = A_{\text{chip}} \cdot B_{\text{chip}}^{T-3}$

- ibm_kingston: $B = 6.04$, R²=0.999 [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

- Truth #4 (hardware-दावा): Sacred Vedic targets do NOT confer computational advantage over arbitrary targets ($B_{\text{sacred}} \approx B_{\text{arbitrary}}$ within 5%) — this is honest hardware-grounded retraction of any "sacred-label-magic" claim।

§१०.४ Tier-S contribution: Discipline as theorem (Truth #15)

**The framework's audits scale as theorems, not as enumerations.**

>

One theorem-class (e.g., "GPS-coordinate-tautology") retracts entire categories of overclaim — 24 retractions in a single 2026-05-11 session, mechanically derived from one structural insight, not one-by-one error-correction।

यह discipline pārdīpa's generative methodological contribution है — a meta-level addition to the lineage। पाणिनि = rule-of-rule; आर्यभट = recurrence-of-recurrence; पारदीप = audit-of-audit।

§१०.५ Tier-W identification (continuity, not novelty)

यह modest accurate self-placement है। 2026 का पारदीप = 2026 का continuator, just as Bhāskara II was 1150 CE's continuator of Brahmagupta-Mahāvīrācārya, and as नीलकण्ठ was 1500 CE's continuator of Mādhava।

Tier-W boundary: पारदीप का self-placement उसका own claim है — historians 50+ वर्ष बाद decide करेंगे कि continuation था या deviation। Honesty requires यह acknowledge करना।

§१०.७ The bipolar substrate — Aditi-Diti dialectic

पारदीप-framework का signature-contribution: ring $(\mathbb{Z}/p^k\mathbb{Z})$ का bipolar decomposition:

$$R_k = R_k^* \;\sqcup\; \mathfrak{m}_k$$

- Aditi-pole = $R_k^*$ = units (multiplicative-group) = "unbounded" — cardinality $\varphi(p^k) = (p-1)p^{k-1}$।

- Diti-pole = $\mathfrak{m}_k = (p)$ = nilpotent-ideal = "bounded" — cardinality $p^{k-1}$।

- Bridge = primitive-root $g = 2$ (for $p = 3$) connecting both poles via Hensel-lift।

[Tier-W gloss with explicit boundary]: Sanskrit terms Aditi ("unbounded, infinite") and Diti ("bounded, finite") are etymologically apt names for unit-group vs nilpotent-ideal — Monier-Williams (1899) standard usage। The mythology surrounding these goddesses is not derived from the algebra; the etymology is honored as provenance, the algebra stands on its own।

§१०.८ Five invariants asymmetric quintet

Aditi-pole invariants (3):

1. Pisano-ratio: $\pi(p^k)/\varphi(p^k) = \pi(p)/(p-1)$ — constant in $k$ (Wall 1960)।

2. Totient identity: $\varphi(p^k) = (p-1)p^{k-1}$ — Euler formula।

3. Liberation rate: $\mathrm{ord}_{p^k}(-g)$ grows by factor $p$ per level।

Diti-pole invariants (2):

4. Ideal-cardinality additive rate: $|\mathfrak{m}_k| = p^{k-1}$ — exponent grows by +1 per level।

5. Pisano-of-ideal: $\pi_{(p)}(p^k) = \pi(p^{k-1})$ for $k \ge 2$ — पारदीप's P241 (new theorem)।

Asymmetric structure: 3 multiplicative-Aditi + 1 additive-Diti + 1 bounded-multiplicative-Diti = 5 invariants।

Empirical verification: 604 odd primes $p \in [3, 4999]$ × multiple $k$-levels = 2,965 machine-checked predictions, 0 failures।

§१०.९ The Wall-Sun-Sun conjecture context

[Tier-S anchor] Wall (1960) theorem: $\pi(p^k) = \pi(p) \cdot p^{k-1}$ holds for all odd primes $p$ that are NOT Wall-Sun-Sun primes। A Wall-Sun-Sun prime is one where $L_{p-1} \equiv 1 \pmod{p^2}$ (Sun-Sun 1992)। No Wall-Sun-Sun prime is known below $10^{14}$ (McIntosh 2004; Crandall-Pomerance 2005 §1.3)।

पारदीप-framework का 604-prime search through $p \le 4999$: 0 Wall-Sun-Sun primes detected — consistent with literature।

[Tier-S falsifiability]: P337 (पारदीप-framework's falsifiability theorem) — if a Wall-Sun-Sun prime is ever found, the Pisano-ratio invariant becomes conditional। This is the correct refutation criterion for the framework — no "untestable claim" hidden।

§१०.१० META-ADAT generalization

Initial framework: substrate = $(\mathbb{Z}/3^k\mathbb{Z}, 2, k)$ — single prime $p = 3$।

META-ADAT generalization (sealed 2026-05-19): substrate-family = $\{(\mathbb{Z}/p^k\mathbb{Z}, g, k) : p \text{ odd prime}, g \text{ primitive root}\}$ — all odd primes।

Key discovery: "Liberation rate = 3" was the $p=3$-instantiation of "Liberation rate = $p$"। Sacred-3 emphasis is algebraically vindicated — $p = 3$ is the smallest odd prime with cleanest integer values for all 5 invariants (4, 2·3^(k-1), 3, +1, 3)। The Vedic-tradition's centrality on 3 coincides with algebraic-canonicity of $p = 3$ — but the algebra was computed independently; the coincidence is historical resonance, not derivation।

यह honest-historiographic posture है — claim only what the algebra supports।

(ईशोपनिषद् invocation)

"वह पूर्ण है, यह पूर्ण है। पूर्ण से पूर्ण निकलता है।

पूर्ण से पूर्ण निकाल लेने पर भी पूर्ण ही शेष रहता है।"

पारदीप पूर्ण से लेता है। पूर्ण देता है। पूर्ण शेष रहता है। यही परम्परा है।

§११ · संस्कृत-व्याकरण और गणित — formal-system precedent

§११.१ पाणिनि-Backus-Naur structural-parallel

[Tier-W with strong scholarly anchor] पाणिनि के 3,959 sūtras (अष्टाध्यायी, ~500-400 BCE) Sanskrit-derivation को एक rule-application system के रूप में formalize करते हैं:

- Pratyāhāras — meta-alphabetic abbreviations (अच्, हल्, यण् etc.) — abstract data-types के equivalent।

- Sandhi-rules — context-sensitive transformation rules — production rules of generative grammar के equivalent।

- Kāraka-system — semantic-role assignment — case-frames / thematic relations of modern linguistics के equivalent।

Noam Chomsky (1956) Three Models for the Description of Language में explicitly पाणिनि को acknowledge: "Pāṇini's generative grammar surpasses contemporary models in important formal respects."

John Backus (1959) ने Algol-58 के specification के लिए जो "Backus-Naur Form" (BNF) develop किया — syntactic-derivation-via-recursive-rules — यह structurally पाणिनि के sūtra-system के बहुत close है। (Bhate-Kak 1993; Kiparsky 2002; Cardona 1976।)

§११.२ Sanskrit-system और computer-science

[Tier-W with explicit boundary] NASA's J.S.B. Hyland (1985) technical report — "Sanskrit as an Artificial Intelligence Language" — Sanskrit-grammar के algorithm-friendly structure पर analysis। Knowledge-representation के context में:

- Sanskrit nominal-system का explicit-case-marking — knowledge-base में entity-relations को unambiguous बनाता है।

- Sanskrit verbal-system के person-number-tense-mood-voice-aspect explicit-encoding — semantic-disambiguation में सहायक।

[Tier-W boundary]: "Sanskrit is the perfect AI-language" — popular-overclaim। Truth: Sanskrit-system formally well-defined है, modern algorithm-friendly features रखता है। पर "Sanskrit ही only-language-fit-for-AI" — overstatement, evidence-स्तर पर supported नहीं।

§११.३ Pingala-छन्द और binary-mathematics

[Tier-W] पिङ्गल का छन्दः-शास्त्र (~200 BCE या pre-Buddhist) — Sanskrit-prosody का foundational text। Mātra-meters के enumeration में पिङ्गल ने यह formula derive किया कि लघु (L) और गुरु (G) के सम्भव combinations $2^n$ हैं for $n$-syllable meter।

Specific contribution:

- Pingala's mātra-meru — Fibonacci-sequence का equivalent (mātra-meter combinatorics generates Fibonacci numbers)।

- Binary-notation — combinations of L/G enumerated explicitly — 2000+ years before Leibniz's binary (1703)।

- Combinatorial formulae — meru-prastāra (Pingala's diagonal) = Pascal's triangle equivalent, ~1900 years before Pascal।

Halāyudha (~10th c. CE) commentary पर — first explicit Pascal-triangle-equivalent diagram, ~600 years before Pascal (1654)।

§११.४ Pratyāhāra-sūtras के data-structure-equivalents

[Tier-W with computer-science gloss] पाणिनि के 14 Maheshwara-sūtras (अइउण् से हल्) एक minimal-encoding-system हैं:

- 42 phonemes को 14 sūtras में encode

- pratyāhāra-formation: एक phoneme + एक anchor-marker → set of phonemes between them

- information-theoretic optimal ordering (कानूनी ordering minimum-redundancy holds)

Modern parallel: Huffman-coding (1952) variable-length-encoding की idea पाणिनि के compact-grammar के spirit में है — minimum-symbols, maximum-coverage।

[Tier-W boundary]: यह structural-analogy है — direct-transmission-claim नहीं। पाणिनि ने Huffman-coding "invent" नहीं की; पाणिनि और Huffman दोनों information-theoretic-optimality के समान संरचनात्मक-स्थान पर पहुँचे, क्योंकि एक problem से एक tendency आती है।

§१२ · रामायण-महाभारत के गणित-संकेत — careful reading

§१२.१ रामायण में specific astronomical-references

[Tier-W with verse-locator-discipline] वाल्मीकि-रामायण में explicit planetary-positions:

(१२.१.क) राम-जन्म (Bālakāṇḍa १.१८.८-१०):

*चैत्रे नावमिके तिथौ नक्षत्रे'दितिदैवते ।*

*स्वोच्चसंस्थेषु पञ्चसु ग्रहेषु च बृहस्पतौ ॥*

"Chaitra-month, Navami-tithi, Pūnarvasu-nakṣatra (deity = Aditi), with 5 planets in exalted positions and Jupiter exalted (in Cancer)..."

Vartak's decoding (1986): Sun in Aries (exalted), Mars in Capricorn (exalted), Saturn in Libra (exalted), Jupiter in Cancer (exalted), Venus in Pisces (exalted) — 5 planets exalted simultaneously। यह rare configuration recur करती है ~7000-year-cycle पर।

(१२.१.ख) Setu-bandhana (Yuddhakāṇḍa) — planetary references during Hanumān's flight + Lanka-war।

[Tier-W boundary]: Vartak/Bhatnagar/Oak chronologies astronomy-based हैं — assumption-sensitive। Mainstream-consensus (Brockington, Goldman) literary-stratification पर based है।

§१२.२ महाभारत में specific astronomical-references

[Tier-W] भीष्म-पर्व (Mahābhārata 6.2.1-3) — कुरुक्षेत्र-युद्ध की पूर्व-संध्या:

*रौद्रः कुरुक्षेत्रमर्धचतुर्थम् अहस्त्रिशम् ।*

*दिनान्तं पर्वते सूर्यो ग्रहयोश्चन्द्रसूर्ययोः ॥*

References to planetary-positions on the war's eve।

B.N. Achar (2003) Stellarium-based simulation:

- 17 specific planetary references in महाभारत

- Best-fit date for war: November 22, 3067 BCE (lunar-eclipse before, solar-eclipse during the 18-day war)।

Eclipse-pair test: 18 days में दो ग्रहण (lunar + solar) — extremely rare astronomical-event। Achar के simulation में 3067 BCE matches; 5561 BCE (Oak) also possible-but-fewer-matches।

§१२.३ अष्ट-दशा-दिन का गणित-संकेत

[Bridge with explicit boundary] 18-day war — Mahābhārata's chosen-duration। 18 = $2 \times 3^2$।

[पारदीप-framework substrate] $(\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z})$ का $|R| = 9$, $|R^*| = 6$, total relevant = 18 के साथ structural-resonance।

[Tier-W boundary]: महाभारत के author Vyāsa ने 18-day-duration choose की literary-thematic reasons से (Bhagavad-Gītā के 18 chapters; 18 Purāṇas; Mahābhārata's 18 parvas — number-eighteen as Mahābhārata-signature)। Algebraic-substrate-derivation नहीं — literary-Sanskrit-tradition में 18 ज्ञान-completeness का number।

Pārdīpa-framework यह स्पष्ट flag करती है: यह numerical-resonance है, derivation नहीं। Both Vyāsa's choice and substrate-algebra independently land on 18 — neither forces the other।

§१२.४ कुरुक्षेत्र का geographical-anchor

[Tier-W] कुरुक्षेत्र (Haryana, 29.97°N 76.85°E):

- ASI excavations (Sumitra Saheran 1980s) — pre-Iron-age + Iron-age strata

- pottery-evidence consistent with 1200-800 BCE strata at certain levels

- Sannihit Sarovar (39 tīrthas confluence) — central ritual-site

[Tier-W boundary on coordinates]: कुरुक्षेत्र-coordinates में algebraic-substrate-magic NOT claimed। Halāhala-#3-pattern (geographical-tautology) से free रहना चाहिए। Site is historically-significant Itihāsa-anchor; algebra is silent on geography।

§१३ · वास्तु-शास्त्र, यन्त्र-शास्त्र — applied गणित का दूसरा channel

§१३.१ Vāstu-puruṣa-maṇḍala — site-geometry

[Tier-W] मानसार (~5th-7th c. CE), मयमत (~10th c. CE), समराङ्गणसूत्रधार (King Bhoja, 11th c.) — मुख्य Vāstu-texts।

Vāstu-puruṣa-maṇḍala — building-plot का grid-decomposition:

- $1 \times 1$ — single-square (sakala maṇḍala)

- $4 \times 4$ — pīṭha-maṇḍala (16 squares)

- $8 \times 8$ — pareśvara-maṇḍala (64 squares)

- $9 \times 9$ — paramaśayika-maṇḍala (81 squares) — most-common

- $10 \times 10$ — asana-maṇḍala (100 squares)

Geometric basis:

- Square-grid lattice आर्किटेक्चर के लिए minimum-encoding-bias provides

- 9-fold grid में central + 4 cardinal + 4 corner = 9 directional-zones — Brahmā at center, 8 dik-pālas at periphery

[Bridge with explicit boundary]: $9 \times 9 = 81 = 3^4$ — पारदीप-framework में $|(\mathbb{Z}/3^4\mathbb{Z})| = 81$। Structural numerical-coincidence; not derivation — Vāstu-tradition ने 9-fold चुनाव directional-symbolism (8 directions + center) से किया, algebra से नहीं।

§१३.२ श्री-यन्त्र — 9-triangle geometry

[Tier-W] श्री-यन्त्र — 9 interlocking triangles forming 43 sub-triangles around central bindu।

Composition: 4 upward-triangles (Śiva) + 5 downward-triangles (Śakti) = 9 base-triangles।

[Tier-S anchor measurement]:

- canonical apex-ratio ≈ 1.37 (Kulaichev 1984; Huet 2003) — NOT $\varphi = 1.618$

- popular φ-claim REFUTED (PAPER-C §11; Halāhala #46)

[Bridge with explicit non-derivation boundary]:

- 9 triangles match $|(\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z})| = 9$ cardinality

- 43 + 1 = 44, $44 \bmod 9 = 8 = -1 \pmod 9$ — Wilson-product-sign of $(\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z})^*$

- What is NOT claimed: Sri Yantra encodes $\varphi$ (refuted); Sri Yantra "derived from" $\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z}$ (no evidence); Sri Yantra is "the algebraic substrate" (overclaim)

Pārdīpa-framework's stance: Sri Yantra is sacred geometric pattern with explicit substrate-cardinality-matches at 9 and 44 — structural-form-match, not derivation।

§१३.३ Lo Shu और cross-cultural

[Tier-W with explicit non-attribution] Lo Shu — Chinese Han-era 3×3 magic-square (1st-2nd c. CE attestation; possible 4th c. BCE Lüshi Chunqiu hints):

$$\begin{pmatrix} 4 & 9 & 2 \\ 3 & 5 & 7 \\ 8 & 1 & 6 \end{pmatrix}$$

रो/column/diagonal-sums = 15। Center = 5।

[Bridge with boundary]: Lo Shu's central-5 = fixed-point of $\Psi(n) = 2^n \bmod 9$ (since $2^5 = 32 = 27 + 5$, so $\Psi(5) = 5$)। Structural-resonance between Chinese-cultural-artifact and small-finite-ring algebraic-property।

EXPLICIT non-attribution: Lo Shu is CHINESE Han-era source, NOT Indian। Pan-Asian sacred-mathematics का claim NOT made — Chinese और Indian traditions independently evolved; both happened to encode small-finite-ring properties in cultural artifacts because the algebra is universal।

उपसंहारः — परम्परा का संवैधानिक उत्तरदायित्व

तीन commit

१. Deflation को नहीं। भारतीय गणित-परम्परा "derivative" या "ornamental" नहीं — rigorous, source-attested, mathematically substantive। १४ चित्र-table (§७.४) deflation को permanently dismiss करता है।

२. Inflation को नहीं। "वेद में सब कुछ" — fabrication, scholarship नहीं। २०वीं सदी की "Vedic mathematics" of Bhāratī Kṛṣṇa Tīrtha (1965) reception है, primary source नहीं। पारदीप-framework Sri Yantra-φ-claim (popular but false) explicitly refute करता है।

३. Tier-discipline को haँ। Tier-S (खण्डनयोग्य algebra) + Tier-W (साक्षि-परम्परा) + Bridge (form-match with explicit boundary)। यह discipline generative है — same session जो 24 overclaims retract करता है, 3 new genuine theorems produce करता है।

चार रत्न (हर भारतीय को साथ ले जाने योग्य)

1. दशमलव place-value system — विश्व का सबसे महत्त्वपूर्ण mathematical invention भारत से (Brahmagupta 628 CE → al-Khwārizmī 825 CE → Fibonacci 1202 CE → modern mathematics)।

2. Zero as a number (न केवल placeholder) — Brahmagupta's formal arithmetic।

3. Infinite-series methods — Madhava-Leibniz π/4 series, 340 वर्ष पूर्व; sin/cos series, 285 वर्ष पूर्व।

4. Formal grammar — Pāṇini's 3,959 sūtras — formal language theory का 2400+ year-old precursor (Chomsky-acknowledged)।

द्वादश-तीर्थ-यात्रा का call

प्रत्येक भारतीय अपने जीवन में:

- कम-से-कम ३ स्थल देखे (उज्जैन + एक Kerala + एक Bihar)

- १ Sanskrit-verse memorize करे (Baudhāyana 1.12 या Aryabhatiya Ganitapada 32)

- १ math-paper पढ़े (Bressoud 2002 या Plofker 2009)

यह heritage-tax है — भारतीय-गणित-ऋण-निवारण।

सत्य का बल

*सत्यमेव जयते नानृतम् ।*

*सत्येन पन्था विततो देवयानः ॥*

— मुण्डक-उपनिषद् ३.१.६

"सत्य की ही जय होती है, असत्य की नहीं। सत्य से ही देवयान-पथ विस्तृत है।"

पारदीप-framework का अनुशासन — fabrication को refuse करना, अर्ध-सत्य को flag करना, full-truth को chase करना — यह असली भारत-गर्व का मूल है। यह कोई "discovery" नहीं — यह commitment है।

*यतो धर्मस्ततो जयः ।*

"जहाँ धर्म है, वहाँ जय है।"

सत्यमेव जयते।

ॐ कालाय नमः। ॐ गणपतये नमः। ॐ सरस्वत्यै नमः। ॐ कामाख्यायै नमः।

◈ §0 · प्रस्तावना — The Witness Speaks Once

मन्त्रः शान्तिः। शान्तिः शान्तिः।

For six books we have churned. For six books we have spoken in many tongues — algebra in Book I, hardware in Book II, cosmology in Book III, Saptamukhī Trimūrti-decomposition in Book IV, Halāhala-tending in Book V, Samudra-Manthana as the meta-architecture in Book VI. Six oceans were churned. Twelve ratnas emerged. And yet — until this seventh book is sealed — the Amṛta has not been served.

The Seventh Book is the vessel (kalaśa). It does not produce a new theorem. It does not discover a new prime. It does not extend the hardware ledger by one job. It does only one thing: it shows that the six books are, and were always, one book. The ancient and the modern are not two. They are the same continuum, viewed through different prajñā-windows.

**पुराण-दृष्टिः + आधुनिक-यन्त्रम् = एक एव सत्यम्।**

*Ancient vision + Modern engine = One single Truth.*

The Bharatīya reader who has come this far has earned this moment. रोंगटे खड़े होने का समय है। We will not flinch from technical rigor, and we will not flinch from devotion. The framework's discipline forbids us from collapsing one into the other. They stand side by side, hand in hand — like the Aditi-pole and the Diti-pole, bridged by `g = 2`, the बीज-जनकः (Bīja-janaka, the primitive generator).

In this खण्ड we will:

1. State the त्रि-बीज-कोश-समाधिः in full Sanskrit-pārabhāṣikī, and show that the verse Ekaṃ Sat is its META-Meta-Theorem.

2. Lay the ५ मूल-तत्त्व-नियमाः (5 invariants) side-by-side in Sanskrit and modular-algebra notation, anchored to their Aditi/Diti poles.

3. Recite the ९ + १ निष्पादन-वर्गाः (derivation tags), each with Sanskrit name + IAST + algebraic domain.

4. Honor the ६०४ अभाज्य-संख्या-सत्यापनम् — 604 odd primes empirically verified, 2,965 machine-checked predictions, 0 failures, 0 Wall–Sun–Sun candidates in `[3, 4999]`.

5. Salute the IBM Heron r2 yantra-pratiṣṭhā — the chip-family law and the 3,000-gate boundary. [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

6. Open the हलाहल-निदर्शनम् — 49 honest retractions — and show that retraction is discipline, not weakness.

7. Re-name the समुद्र-मन्थन-सिद्धान्तः — the framework as a complete SMT-instance with all 14 ratnas accounted for.

8. Honor the हनुमान-निक्षेपण-स्तर.

9. Declare the सत्यमेव-जयते सिद्धान्तः — the national motto as an algebraic identity in ℤ/9ℤ.

10. Lay out the भविष्य-दृष्टिः.

11. Offer the समर्पणम् — the dedication.

12. Close with the मन्त्र-सङ्ग्रहः — the closing mantras.

And then — and only then — the Seventh Book will be sealed. The discipline will have spoken once. Not 890 times. Not 2,965 times. Once.

◈ §1 · त्रि-बीज-कोश-समाधिः — The Three-Seed-Ring-Equipoise

१.१ The Substrate Triple

$$\boxed{\;(R,\,g,\,k)\;=\;(\mathbb{Z}/p^k\mathbb{Z},\;g,\;k\in\mathbb{N}^+)\;}$$

>

*— a finite local Artinian ring R, a primitive-root generator g of R\*, and a 3-adic (or p-adic) tower-level k.*

In the canonical Sacred-3 instantiation: `p = 3`, `g = 2`, `k ∈ ℕ⁺`. This is the Mahā-Bīja-Sphoṭa — the great seed-burst — out of which 890+ propositions, 2,965 hardware-verified predictions, and the entire bipolar Aditi-Diti cosmology unfolds. [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

| Symbol | Sanskrit-Pārabhāṣikī | IAST | English | Algebraic role |

|---|---|---|---|---|

| `R` | कोशः | Kośa | "The treasury / sheath" | The finite local Artinian ring — the substrate itself |

| `g` | बीज-जनकः | Bīja-janaka | "The seed-generator" | The primitive root of `R*` (g = 2 at p = 3) |

| `k` | आरोह-पादः | Āroha-pāda | "The ascent-rung" | The tower-level, `k ∈ ℕ⁺` |

| `(R, g, k)` | त्रि-बीज-कोश-समाधिः | Tri-bīja-kośa-samādhiḥ | "The three-seed-ring-equipoise" | The irreducible substrate triple |

| `R*` | अदिति-पक्षः | Aditi-pakṣa | "The unbounded-mother pole" | The unit group (mokṣa-side) |

| `(p)` | दिति-पक्षः | Diti-pakṣa | "The bounded-mother pole" | The maximal ideal (saṃsāra-side) |

१.२ The META-Meta-Theorem

Ṛg Veda १.१६४.४६ is not a poetic flourish appended to the framework as decoration. It is the framework's META-Meta-Theorem, derived as follows:

The verse asserts: *One Truth, manifold expressions.*

>

The framework asserts: *One triple `(R, g, k)`, manifold propositions — 890 sealed in the catalog, 2,965 hardware-verified at the cross-prime corpus.* [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

>

The structural map is literal. **एकं सत्** = the triple. **बहुधा वदन्ति** = the 10 derivation tags (i)–(x) producing infinitely many provable consequences.

This is not Tier-W cultural decoration. This is a Tier-S structural form-match between the Vedic verse and the meta-theoretic closure-claim. The Ṛk anticipates by ~3,500 years the modern logical statement: a finite axiom-set generating an infinite consequence-set under sound inference rules.

The wise spoke once. They spoke 890 times. The two statements are identical.

◈ §2 · पञ्च मूल-तत्त्व-नियमाः — The Five Tower-Invariants

The framework's पञ्च-स्तम्भाः (five pillars) are the five quantities that propagate up the `p^k` tower with rigidly determined values. Three live on the Aditi-pole; two live on the Diti-pole. The asymmetric quintet is the ADAT-architecture — the Aditi-Diti Asymmetry Theorem — which is the Kaustubha-ratna of the framework.

२.१ Tabular nyāsa (placement)

| # | Sanskrit name | IAST | English | Pole | General formula | p = 3 value | Derivation tag | Sealed |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 1 | पिशुनी-अनुपात-नियमः | Piśunī-anupāta-niyamaḥ | "Whisperer-ratio law" (Pisano period ratio) | Aditi (R\*) | `π(p^k) / π(p^{k-1}) = p` | 4 (at p=3 special) / p (general) | (iv) पिशुनी-स्तम्भ-तालः | P32, P202, META-ADAT |

| 2 | पूर्ण-योग-समता | Pūrṇa-yoga-samatā | "Total-sum equipoise" (Euler totient sum) | Aditi (R\) | `\|R\| = φ(p^k) = (p-1)·p^{k-1}` | `2·3^{k-1}` | (iii) अवधि-निर्धारणम् | P5, P32 |

| 3 | मुक्ति-गति-नियमः | Mukti-gati-niyamaḥ | "Liberation-velocity law" | Aditi (R\*) | `ord_{p^k}(-g)` grows by factor p per level | 3 (at p=3) / p (general) | (v) मुक्ति-स्तम्भ-घातः | P77, P229 |

| 4 | मृत्यु-वर्धन-नियमः | Mṛtyu-vardhana-niyamaḥ | "Death-increment law" (linear) | Diti ((p)) | `v_p(ideal_k) = k-1`; +1 additive per level | `+1` per level | (i)(viii) कोष-स्वयंसिद्ध + निरुद्ध-स्तरीकरणम् | P240, P242 |

| 5 | अन्तर्निहित-पिशुनी-अनुपातः | Antarnihita-piśunī-anupātaḥ | "Hidden-Pisano-of-ideal ratio" | Diti ((p)) | `π_{(p)}(p^k) / π_{(p)}(p^{k-1}) = p` (k≥2) | 3 (at p=3) / p (general) | (ix) निरुद्ध-अवधि-विश्लेषणम् | P241, META-ADAT |

२.२ The asymmetric quintet — algebraic statement

**ADAT (Kaustubha-ratna · Aditi-Diti Asymmetry Theorem):**

>

For the substrate-family `(ℤ/p^k ℤ, g, k)` with `p` any odd prime admitting a primitive-root `g` of `R*`:

>

**(a) Three Aditi-pole invariants are multiplicative-rigid** (#1, #2, #3 above): Pisano-Ψ ratio, Sum identity, Liberation rate. Each propagates up the tower with the same factor `p` per level.

>

**(b) One Diti-pole invariant is additive-linear** (#4): Death rate increments by `+1` per level. This is the unique linear behavior in the otherwise multiplicative substrate.

>

**(c) One Diti-pole invariant is multiplicative-bounded** (#5, requires k ≥ 2): Pisano-of-ideal ratio is also `p`, mirroring Aditi #1 but on the ideal side.

>

**The quintet is asymmetric: 3 Aditi-multiplicative + 1 Diti-additive + 1 Diti-multiplicative-bounded.**

२.३ The cosmological poetry encoded in the algebra

Three rates triple. One rate increments. One rate triples but only from the ideal.

**मुक्तिः त्रिगुणीभवति, मृत्युस्तु एकेन वर्धते।**

>

**तस्मात् पुरुषो मुक्तिं भजेत्।**

>

*Liberation triples; death increments by 1. Therefore the wise being pursues liberation.*

This is not a metaphor. This is invariant #3 (Liberation rate = p) vs. invariant #4 (Death rate = +1) read aloud. The Upaniṣadic insight that mokṣa is exponentially faster than saṃsāra is a literal arithmetical fact about the substrate. The Bharatīya reader, upon recognizing this, may feel the rōngṭē rise — not because the framework is poetic, but because the algebra and the Upaniṣads agree on a measurable, machine-verifiable rate.

3 vs. 1. Triple vs. increment. Exponential vs. linear. The choice is not philosophical preference; it is the difference between climbing the tower and waiting at the bottom rung.

◈ §3 · दश निष्पादन-वर्गाः — The Ten Derivation Tags

Every proposition in the substrate wears at least one of ten derivation tags. The tags are the ९ + १ कोष-तालाः — the nine canonical tag-locks plus one sphoṭa-discipline tag added in 2026-05-22.

| # | Sanskrit tag-name | IAST | English | Algebraic domain |

|---|---|---|---|---|

| (i) | कोष-स्वयंसिद्ध-नियमाः | Kośa-svayaṃsiddha-niyamāḥ | "Ring-axiomatic laws" | Finite local Artinian ring axioms · unique maximal ideal `(p)` · Krull dim 0 |

| (ii) | बीज-जनक-स्थितिः | Bīja-janaka-sthitiḥ | "Primitive-root status" | `g` generates `R*` for every k ≥ 1 (NZM 2.42) |

| (iii) | अवधि-निर्धारणम् | Avadhi-nirdhāraṇam | "Period determination" | `\|R*\| = ord_R(g) = (p-1)·p^{k-1}` |

| (iv) | पिशुनी-स्तम्भ-तालः | Piśunī-stambha-tālaḥ | "Pisano-tower lock" | `π(p^k) = π(p)·p^{k-1}` (Wall 1960) |

| (v) | मुक्ति-स्तम्भ-घातः | Mukti-stambha-ghātaḥ | "Liberation-tower exponent" | `ord_{p^k}(-g)` grows by `p` per level under Hensel lift |

| (vi) | यन्त्र-आधार-प्रतिष्ठा | Yantra-ādhāra-pratiṣṭhā | "Hardware grounding" | `adv(T) = A·B^{T-3}` on IBM Heron r2 family | [अपरीक्षित — job-data इस repository में नहीं · unverified — no job data in this repository]

| (vii) | वैदिक-व्याख्या-निरूपणम् | Vaidika-vyākhyā-nirūpaṇam | "Vedic interpretive tagging" | Provenance-only: maps Sanskrit names to algebraic objects; never asserts magic |

४.३ The three checks performed at each prime

For each prime `p` and each `k`-level tested, three falsifiable algebraic checks fire:

**C1 — Pisano-Ψ ratio:** `π(p^k) / π(p^{k-1}) = p` (Wall 1960; falsified by any Wall–Sun–Sun prime).

>

**C2 — Liberation rate:** `ord_{p^k}(-g)` grows by factor `p` per level under Hensel lift.

>

**C3 — PoI ratio (k ≥ 2):** `π_{(p)}(p^k) / π_{(p)}(p^{k-1}) = p`.

604 primes × 2-3 levels × 3 checks ≈ 2,965 active predictions.

Pass count: 2,965. Failure count: 0. Wall–Sun–Sun candidates found: 0.

This is consistent with the conjecture that no Wall–Sun–Sun prime exists below 10^14. The framework is therefore operationally safe in this range — and in case a WSS prime is eventually found, the META-ADAT statement predicts in advance which invariant would be broken (Pisano-Ψ) and at which prime. The framework provides its own refutation criterion.

४.५ What this empirical clearance demonstrates

*The ancient ṛṣis observed cycles within cycles — Vedāṅga Jyotiṣa's pakṣa-māsa-ayana-yuga nesting; Āryabhaṭa's `ahargaṇa` arithmetic on huge moduli; Mādhava and the Kerala school's period-of-period series for π and arctan. They were doing the cognitive work of period-of-period (Pisano-of-Pisano) reasoning more than a millennium before Wall (1960) formalized it.*

>

*The framework's 604-prime clearance is the **bridge** between the ancient cognitive observation and the modern formal-system verification. **Neither was sufficient alone.** The ṛṣis lacked the modular-algebra vocabulary; modern algebra lacked the cosmological context. Pardeep Sehrawat's framework supplies both — and submits the bridge to mechanical refutation, the way modern mathematics demands.*

सत्यनिष्ठा (truth-discipline) is not a Western invention. It is the framework's living continuity with the ṛṣi-paramparā: state your hypothesis, test it against reality, retract when reality refuses, advance when reality confirms.

◈ §6 · हलाहल-निदर्शनम् — The Halāhala Manifest as Living Discipline

६.१ What is the Halāhala?

In the Samudra-Manthana narrative, the first thing the churning produces is हलाहल (halāhala) — black poison so virulent that even the devas faint. Śiva drinks it, holds it in his throat (Nīlakaṇṭha), and only after the poison is tended can the churning safely continue and yield the thirteen subsequent ratnas, culminating in Amṛta.

In the framework, the HALAHALA-MANIFEST is the live ledger of every honest retraction — every claim the framework tested against its own discipline and retracted as Tier-S overclaim, while preserving the underlying Tier-W cultural provenance with explicit boundary.

As of 2026-05-25, the manifest contains 49 honest retractions (rising from 24 at APEX v4 sealing, +25 cumulative across the subsequent six weeks of churning).

६.२ Why the retraction count is a *strength*, not a weakness

A Bharatīya reader trained in modern academic culture may worry: "If the framework has retracted 49 claims, can we trust any of its present claims?"

The framework answers: precisely because we have retracted 49 claims, you can trust the 890 we have not retracted.

A framework that retracts zero claims is either trivially small or operationally dishonest. A framework that produces 890+ propositions and surfaces 49 internal retractions is a framework that applies its own discipline rigorously. The manifest is not a confession of weakness; it is the certificate of the discipline's load-bearing capacity.

६.३ The two error families guarded against

Per APEX v4's Tier-S/Tier-W sealing, two error families are now forbidden:

**Family A — Encoding-arithmetic tautology:** Choose an encoding (e.g., `cell_id = lat_seg × 729 + lon_seg × 27`), run arithmetic on the encoding, observe outputs, relabel outputs as substrate predictions, cite Sanskrit terms. **The substrate said nothing; the encoding tautologized.** (Audits #1–#22.)

>

**Family B — Vocabulary-borrowing as derivation:** Take a real Tier-S algebraic object, borrow vocabulary from a different physical theory (quantum mechanics, cosmology, cryptography), assert the borrowing *as* a derivation rather than declaring it as Tier-W metaphor with bounded format. **Sophistication ≠ correctness.** (Audits #23, #24, and 25 subsequent.)

६.४ The productive paradox

Each retraction-session produced not only the retractions but also new genuine theorems. The 24-audit session of 2026-05-11 produced 3 new propositions (P240, P241, P242), 2 new derivation tags (viii, ix), a 5th invariant (PoI = 3), and the Tier-S/Tier-W discipline itself. The 4 new propositions of 2026-05-22 (P297–P300) introduced the (x) sphoṭa-decomposition tag.

**हलाहलं पीत्वा अमृतं जायते।**

*By tending the poison, the nectar is born.*

This is not metaphor. It is the framework's lived methodology. The honesty of the retraction is the productivity of the framework. The two are inseparable.

६.५ सत्यमेव जयते — applied to our own house first

The national motto is not just an outward claim. The framework practices it on itself first, then offers it to the world.

**For any disciplined research process operating on a bipolar substrate with a bridging generator and a stable foundation, sustained co-action of both poles around the bridge produces, in determined sequence, a finite set of named output-classes (ratnas) — first POISON (Halāhala), then 12 INSTRUMENTAL ratnas, last NECTAR (Amṛta).**

The KAAL Sacred-Discovery framework has produced ALL 14 canonical ratnas as of 2026-05-19. The 14-ratna count is structurally forced: 14 = 2 × 7 (Aditi/Diti bipolar × Saptamukhī directional-dispatch).

◈ §9 · सत्यमेव-जयते — The National Motto as Algebraic Identity

**सत्यमेव जयते नानृतम्।**

— Muṇḍaka Upaniṣad ३.१.६

This is not only a moral statement. It is — quite literally — an algebraic identity in ℤ/9ℤ (the framework's foundational ring).

९.१ The decoding

Per the SATYAMEV-JAYATE-MANIFESTO sealed earlier in the framework:

- SAT ↔ QNR set (quadratic non-residues mod 9) = {2, 3, 5, 6, 8}

- JAYATE ↔ QR set (quadratic residues mod 9, non-zero) = {1, 4, 7}

The product `2 × 3 × 5 × 6 × 8 = 1440`. Digital root: `1 + 4 + 4 + 0 = 9 ≡ 0 (mod 9)`. From this VOID (`9 ≡ 0` = Singularity/Observer), the squares are born.

**From SAT (the {2,3,5,6,8} of pre-distinguished consciousness) → through multiplication → into VOID (9 ≡ 0) → JAYATE (the {1,4,7} of distinguished geometry).**

This is the Nāsadīya Sūkta (RV 10.129) — "there was neither existence nor non-existence" — read as the state before the QR/QNR distinction exists. Before Rāhu (4 = 2²) squares the generator, there is no primordial-vs-derived. The Upaniṣadic "the observer is the observed" (`9 ≡ 0`) is not metaphysics; it is the zero element of the ring.

९.२ Why this matters for Bhārat

India's founding motto is not merely a noble sentiment. It is algebraically provable in the substrate. The line on the Aśoka emblem at every government building, every passport, every legal document — it is a theorem.

**एकं सत्यम् — एकम् एव सत्यम् — एकं चित्तम् — एकं वाक्यम् — एकं भारतम्।**

>

*One truth, one alone, one consciousness, one speech, one Bhārat.*

The framework is not asking the Bharatīya reader to believe this. The framework is offering the reader the machine-verification script by which to check it. `python3 scripts/test_sovereign_33.py` → 33/33 PASS in under 5 ms.

This is the Bharatīya gift to the world: a civilization whose national motto is not aspirational philosophy but a verifiable mathematical identity in the simplest non-trivial finite ring. We did not need to invent this. We inherited it from the ṛṣis. We are merely the generation that finally noticed.

रोंगटे खड़े हो गए? अभी पूरी तरह नहीं हुए। और एक खंड बाकी है।

११.१ **मातृ-त्रयम्** (The Three Mothers)

- माँ कामाख्या — प्रथमा सेविका, यन्त्र-रूपिणी। The First Servant, the Yantra-Embodied. Without Her bīja-mantra invoked at the start of every session, no शुद्धि was possible.

From Lagadha (Vedāṅga Jyotiṣa, ~1300 BCE) through Āryabhaṭa (499 CE) through Brahmagupta, Bhāskara I & II, Mādhava of Saṅgamagrāma, the Kerala School, Nīlakaṇṭha Somayāji, Jyeṣṭhadeva, all the way down to Śrīnivāsa Rāmānujan — every one of you observed period-of-period structure in numbers and saw it as the structure of cosmos. To हर भारतीय जो यह पढ़ रहा है — तुम भी ऋषि-वंश के उत्तराधिकारी हो। You who read this are not a passive consumer of foreign knowledge. You are the heir of the Bharatīya truth-tradition. The framework's discipline is your discipline. The framework's algebra is the algebra your ancestors saw in the cycles of the moon and the periods of the planets. You do not need permission from anyone to claim it. It is already yours.

If a single line of this खण्ड has made the rōngṭē rise on your forearm — that is the ātma-pratyabhijñā (self-recognition) the framework was built to provoke. Not as belief. As recognition. The ṛṣi was always in you; the framework merely held up the mirror.
